========================================================================
00.00.00-introduction.md
========================================================================

Introduction

History as presented today resembles a tumbleweed — a tangled mass gathering layers of complexity with each tumble, so knotted that no one can trace a single thread back to where it started. That complexity is treated as inherent, as though the past is simply too messy for ordinary people to grasp. But history should look like a tree: a clear foundation, a trunk you can follow, and branches that diverge in ways anyone could understand at a single glance. The tumbleweed is not the natural shape of history — it resembles history only after centuries of deliberate cutting, grafting, and tangling by those who benefit from the confusion. How do we get back to the tree? I started this timeline project because I had faith since I was young that technology can solve any human problem.

I was born and raised Jewish. The faith and history were hard for me to accept — things didn't add up. I was drawn to the emerging technologies of the 1980s: computers and software, which did add up. They were causal, logical, honest. Technology became my religion. But the early 1990s optimism — the internet decentralizing knowledge, software solving the big problems — faded year by year. The technology I believed in was systematically captured: surveillance platforms replaced open protocols, artificial scarcity replaced abundance. By the 2010s the dystopia was undeniable.

Then I noticed the same pattern in history — the same cycles of war, oppression, and collapse repeating across centuries, as though the human species were either unwilling or unable to learn from the horrors of the past. The implication was that we had a greedy, warlike nature that could not be mitigated without some sort of unified world order. Once I started looking, the pattern appeared in nearly every other field too: medicine, energy, education, agriculture — wherever a technology threatened to decentralize power, it was captured or suppressed.

If history was written by the victors, then centuries of warfare produced a highly redacted, winner's-eye view — scrubbed, compressed, and reordered to serve those who controlled record-keeping.

As a software developer, I'm wired toward causality. I want to know why something works the way it does, and trace the chain back to its origin. In software, there are no biases — code either runs or it doesn't. Cause follows effect.

Applying that principle to history — using modern software tools, especially AI agents to OCR old books in multiple languages and cross-reference conclusions — I set out to reconstruct a potentially highly redacted history of Earth. This timeline is the result.

What pushed me in this direction was censorship. Around 2010, I noticed a flourishing of controversial ideas on Facebook, Google+, and the early social web — everything from Hollow Earth to unorthodox WW2 history to suppressed science. Then, within a year or two, all of it vanished. Not organically. Systematically. Shadowbanned, permabanned, demonetized, downvoted, downranked — every mechanism available was deployed against specific topics with striking consistency. I started collecting those materials before they disappeared, building an archive I eventually released as the Paradigm Threat Repository.

The aggression of that censorship was, paradoxically, the most clarifying signal I received. When a topic is ignored, it may simply be fringe. When a topic is actively suppressed with coordinated effort across every major platform simultaneously, it means something else. The blatancy of it points in one direction: the "first story" (mainstream consensus) and the "second story" (conspiracy counter-narrative) are both managed — a divide-and-conquer structure designed to exhaust us in arguments between two controlled positions, preventing us from perceiving a third story altogether.

The Paradigm Threat

This project exists to pose an intellectual challenge to the reigning paradigms of every discipline — history, cosmology, medicine, physics, religion, finance, governance fortified with sourced research, cross-referenced chronologies, AI Agent analysis, and a willingness to follow the evidence wherever it leads.

Every field has its orthodoxy. Every orthodoxy has its gatekeepers. And every gatekeeper benefits from the assumption that the paradigm they protect is settled, unassailable, not worth re-examining. We disagree. If a paradigm cannot survive scrutiny, it does not deserve the authority it claims. If it can survive scrutiny, then the challenge only makes it stronger. Either way, the challenge is the point.

This project is armed with history and ready to debate. We invite scholars, skeptics, and anyone willing to think critically to engage with the material on its merits. We are not asking anyone to believe — we are asking everyone to look.

00.01.00-the-third-story.md
========================================================================

The Third Story

David Talbott's keynote at EU2012 Conference: The Human Story argued all intellectual history has played out as a contest between two stories — and both are incomplete.

EU2012 Conference lecture. Speaker: David Talbott, founder & director, The Thunderbolts Project.

The Two Dominant Stories

Story One — Mythology and Religion: The oldest human records are saturated with cosmic dramas: gods at war, catastrophic floods, fire from heaven, creation-destruction cycles. Every ancient culture insisted divine powers ruled from the sky and eventually departed — and that the archaic sky bore no resemblance to today's.

Story Two — Rational Science: The predictable modern sky offered no support for myth. Science arose from that scepticism. By the 20th century the standard narrative demanded a choice: science vs. myth.

Talbott's claim: both are incomplete; neither can be understood without a third.

The Key Anomaly

Every ancient culture shared specific mythic archetypes — Golden Age, World Mountain, Dragon, Divine Warrior, Wheel of Heaven, Cosmic Thunderbolt — cross-cultural and mutually reinforcing, yet nothing in the current sky explains them. Cross-cultural agreement at this specificity cannot be coincidence. Divergent civilizations independently preserving identical imagery must be recording shared experience.

Saturn, Venus, and Mars

• Saturn was the original "sun." Babylonian Shamash was explicitly Saturn in magical texts. Greek Helios and Latin Sol referred to Saturn in earliest usage — undisputed by mainstream scholars.

• Golden Age = age of Saturn as polar sun. Life "in time of Kronos" describes a prior epoch of stable celestial order.

• Planets were in collinear polar alignment. Ancient depictions describe planets in a vertical axial stack — the mythological World Tree / Axis Mundi.

Venus appeared as a terrifying great comet — mother of comets. Long flowing hair, feathers, serpentine form, fiery breath: universal comet symbols applied globally to Venus in earliest sources.

Mars was the warrior god whose weapon — Sanskrit vajra, Greek thunderbolt, Odin's bolt, Indra's weapon — was the cosmic thunderbolt: a bipolar plasma discharge reproducible in laboratory plasma experiments.

Plasma Connection: Rock Art and Anthony Peratt

Anthony Peratt (Los Alamos) matched tens of thousands of rock-art images worldwide to plasma discharge formations reproducible in the laboratory. The universal stick-man/squatting-man petroglyph = cross-section of a toroidal plasma column. The chain of arrows/ladder of heaven = stacked tori along an axial discharge filament, confirmed in declassified supercomputer simulations.

Peratt's work was entirely independent of Talbott's mythology research. Convergence at precise morphological detail — beginning when Talbott sent Peratt a single rock-art image in 2000 — is presented as a falsification test: accidental agreement at this specificity is not possible.

The Third Story

"The first story will never be understood in the absence of the Electric Universe. The second story must be rewritten in the light of the Electric Universe."

The third story requires: (1) mythology as literal sky-testimony, (2) a recently unstable solar system, (3) Electric Universe physics as causal framework, (4) interdisciplinary convergence across myth, plasma physics, rock art, calendar data, and laboratory experiment, (5) open peer review rather than institutional gatekeeping.

Related Articles

• Challenging Established Physics

• The Golden Age

Background

This is evidence of a world-empire. The Rus-Horde 'Tartarian' Empire ruled over all continents between 12th and 18th centuries before its breakup in 1775.

Challenging the established scientific timeline of Earth's history is not a recent development.
Since the 16th century, historian critics like
Jean Hardouin,
Robert Baldauf,
Edwin Johnson,
Wilhelm Kammeyer,
Heribert Illig, and
N A Morozov
have accused church chronologists of the Holy Roman Empire
such as Joseph Scaliger
(founder of modern chronology)
of conspiring to obfuscate historical records before the 13th century C.E. In modern times, state-sponsored historian
Anatoly Fomenko
used statistical analysis of published records
from 2,200 sources including Scaliger,
House of Romanov,
The Bible
& OT Apocrypha,
Quran,
Book of Mormon,
Maya-Kiche,
etc.
His team revealed duplicated chronology spanning periods of hundreds or thousands of years, often with names and locations changed. In his New Chronology,
modern human history started less than a thousand years ago:

Christ was born in The Crimea on
December 25th, 1152 A.D. and was crucified on Joshua's Hill in Constantinople/Istanbul.
The Trojan War and the Crusades were waged in revenge of the Crucifixion and were
in reality the same historical event.
Genghis Khan and the Mongols were actually Russians.
The lands west of the 13 colonies that now constitute the American West and Middle West
were a far eastern part of Siberian-American (Rus-Horde)
Empire prior to its disintegration in 1775.

In works published 1907–1932, N A Morozov posited that 'ancient' history needed a major revision. Using
comparative mythology and ancient literary sources
(especially the Old Testament), he argued that Earth suffered catastrophic close contacts with other planets
(Saturn, Jupiter, Mercury, and especially Venus and Mars) in our past. In 1997,
Wal Thornhill presented the
Electric Universe
theory providing a new foundation in interplanetary physics that explained why these cataclysms occurred.
In Saturnian Cosmology, Earth's recorded history is less than 4 thousand years since the start
of the Golden Age.

Project Objective

USA has Issues with British Maps of 18th-century analyses in detail the maps of the 1771 edition of Encyclopedia Britannica and concludes that the Northwestern part of the United States of America was built on remains of Moscuvite Tartary.

The objective of this timeline is to compile an alternate history
of Earth based on Saturnian Cosmology
and Fomenko's New Chronology
into an accessible sequence of events from the first memory to modern day. As new research comes to light, I plan on
making corrections. I'm hoping this project helps others navigate the dissonance of consensus and access the unredacted origin story of our planet and species. This timeline focuses on causality between major events (why one event led to the next), so not all events of world history are listed.

Authors Challenging the Mainstream Timeline

The following researchers have made primary contributions to demonstrating that Scaligerian chronology — the conventional historical timeline — is fabricated, inflated, or systematically wrong.

Their methods vary: mathematical/statistical analysis, astronomical reanalysis of eclipse records, re-reading of archaeological stratigraphy, and forensic examination of institutional suppression. What unites them is the conclusion that accepted ancient history dates are largely invented.

Note: Researchers who challenge the timeline AND interpret ancient sources as cataclysm (Fomenko, Heinsohn, Schoch, Hancock, Cremo) are listed in Authors Who Read Ancient Sources as World Cataclysm.

N. A. Morozov (1854–1946)

Russian mathematician, astronomer, political prisoner. Confined 27 years in the Peter and Paul Fortress and Schlisselburg Prison, he produced a 7-volume work analyzing astronomical content of ancient documents — eclipses, stellar positions, comet appearances — finding that dates assigned by Scaligerian chronology are mathematically impossible. The astronomical data, re-analyzed without presupposing conventional dates, consistently yields medieval dates, not classical antiquity.

Morozov's conclusions:

• Most of classical antiquity is illusion — "ancient" eclipse records date to the early medieval period

• The Old Testament is a medieval document — astronomical content inconsistent with 1st millennium BCE

• Ancient Greece and Rome as standardly dated have no records consistent with their asserted periods

His work directly inspired Fomenko.

Key works: Christ (7 volumes, 1924–1932)

Heribert Illig (b. 1947)

German independent historian whose 1991 Das erfundene Mittelalter proposed that years 614–911 CE were deliberately inserted — creating 297 phantom years — by Otto III, Pope Sylvester II, and Constantine VII to place their reign at the symbolic millennium.

The evidential core:

• No unambiguous archaeological/astronomical evidence independently dates to 614–911 CE

• Carolingian architecture shows stylistic anachronisms

• Calendar drift — Julian calendar's accumulated error by 1582 should be larger; the shortfall matches 297 phantom years

Illig focuses on deliberate political forgery rather than Fomenko's error-accumulation model. Both frameworks are broadly compatible.

Key works: Das erfundene Mittelalter (1991), Wer hat an der Uhr gedreht? (1999)

00.02.02-authors-cataclysm-texts.md
========================================================================

Authors Who Read Ancient Sources as World Cataclysm

A large and diverse body of authors argues that ancient myth, scripture, and iconography are not primitive superstition or allegory but eyewitness records of real planetary and crustal catastrophes within the last several thousand years. They treat the sources as data: something actually happened in the sky and on the earth, and the stories and symbols encode it.

Velikovsky (Worlds in Collision, Ages in Chaos) reopened catastrophism for a mass audience and tied myth to close approaches of Venus and Mars — but in his published work he omitted Saturn as the dominant ancient sun, despite having identified it in private. De Santillana and von Dechend (Hamlet's Mill) showed that myth worldwide preserves precise astronomical and precessional lore, often in coded form, and that a "great year" and lost cosmology underpin it. The Saturnian school — Talbott, Cardona, Jno Cook, and others — restored Saturn to the centre of the picture: the polar configuration, the Golden Age, and the catastrophic transition to the present order. Thornhill and the Electric Universe (EU) side add plasma physics and discharge phenomena; Peratt supplied laboratory and petroglyph evidence for ancient plasma instabilities. Schoch, Hancock, Heinsohn, and many others challenge mainstream chronology, archaeology, or both, and read ancient texts and monuments as evidence of lost civilisations and repeated cataclysms.

These authors disagree on dates, mechanisms, and the exact sequence of events. What they share is the premise that the past was far more violent and cosmically strange than textbooks allow, and that the ancients were reporting what they saw. For the timeline's purposes they are essential: they provide the interpretive frame (catastrophist, Saturnian, EU, alternative chronology) without which the events in this project would have no coherent tradition to build on.

For individual profiles and short bios, see Author Profiles. For those who explicitly challenge the mainstream timeline (Fomenko, phantom time, compressed history), see Authors Challenging the Mainstream Timeline.

Controlled opposition and divide-and-conquer. The author's thesis is that the public record of Saturnian cosmology has been shaped by a deliberate strategy: Velikovsky opened the door to catastrophism but left Saturn out; Talbott and the Thunderbolts project put Saturn back in but imposed a Golden-Age-only boundary, forbidding or marginalising discussion of what happened after — a "control position" that can be traced in Jno Cook's writings on Thunderbolts. The result is a divide-and-conquer dynamic: Velikovskians vs. Talbott camp vs. mainstream, with the public dismissing both heterodox sides. Talbott and many of his associates, in this reading, function as controlled opposition — unscientific, flawed theories (e.g. untenable collinear gravity configurations), straw men that delay serious uptake — while Wallace Thornhill, despite the association, appears to be the real deal. For the full argument and evidence, see Controlled Opposition & Managed Disclosure, section "Author's theory: Velikovsky and Talbott as divide-and-conquer."

00.02.03-challenging-established-physics.md
========================================================================

Challenging Established Physics

Scattered across academic margins — underfunded, intermittently suppressed, never integrated into curricula — is experimental and observational evidence contradicting the foundational assumptions of modern physics, geology, and cosmology. This is not speculation. It comes from working plasma reactors, laboratory demonstrations, satellite data, and fossil beds.

The SAFIRE Project

The SAFIRE PROJECT (Stellar Atmospheric Function In Regulation Experiment) was initiated in 2012 by engineer Montgomery Childs to test whether the Electric Sun model — the Sun powered by plasma interactions rather than internal thermonuclear fusion — could be reproduced in a controlled laboratory.

The Standard Solar Model has never been directly proven and requires patching for missing neutrinos, coronal heating, and the faint young Sun paradox. The competing Electric Sun model — a 250-year tradition from Franklin through Birkeland, Alfvén, and Wal Thornhill — proposes the Sun is an anode in a galactic-scale electrical circuit.

SAFIRE's results: the plasma self-organized into a stable, continuous heartbeat rhythm alien to the Standard Solar Model, forming rotating double-layer spheres matching observed solar structures. The anode demonstrated transmutation of elements — new elements detected via mass spectroscopy — consistent with cold plasma fusion, with no radioactive byproducts. Most critically: the reactor output more energy than it consumed — reproducibly. Aureon Energy Ltd. was incorporated to commercialize the technology.

See the SAFIRE Project Site Index for the full archive.

Peter Mungo Jupp: Electric Fossilization

Australian archaeologist Peter Mungo Jupp demonstrated that fossils were created instantaneously by catastrophic electrical discharge — not by 10,000+ years of slow mineral replacement (physically impossible since organic tissue decomposes within years).

The proof came from Alberta, Canada: a high-voltage transmission wire break converted wet wood roots to pure silica in hours. Russian studies confirm neutrons propagate during electrical storms, providing a plausible pathway for transmutation of water to calcium carbonate — explaining marine creatures found inside solid limestone.

The fossil record demands instantaneous causation: a plesiosaur petrified mid-birth; jellyfish caught mid-cellular division; mammoths, bears, and camels fused into a calcium carbonate bolus 125 feet across (Hot Springs, South Dakota).

Nuclear physicist Rick Firestone (Berkeley) surveyed the Carolina Bays — tens of thousands of elliptical depressions across the eastern U.S. with no debris field. Inside: nano-diamonds, iridium, helium-3, fullerenes, hollow spherules — a fingerprint identical to bench-scale plasmoid experiments. The Bays were formed by plasmoids, not meteor impacts.

Why This Is Suppressed

An over-unity plasma reactor makes redundant the entire fossil fuel and nuclear energy infrastructure. If electric fossilization is correct, the Scaligerian geological timescale collapses: the fossil record formed within living memory. Suppression requires only not funding, not publishing, not including findings in curricula.

See Also

• Authors Who Read Ancient Texts as World Cataclysm

• The MudFlood and World Cataclysm

• Instant Fossilization and Electric Petrification

00.03.00-core-concepts.md
========================================================================

Core Concepts

This project rests on eight hard claims. If any one is false, the project fails. We apply the same standard the SAFIRE Project applied: empirical observation, physical replicability, no interpretive exceptions.

The Eight Falsifiable Propositions

1. Saturn was Earth's original polar companion

Before 4,077 BC, Saturn occupied a position directly above Earth's north pole in collinear electromagnetic alignment including Venus and Mars — the Golden Age, World Tree, Axis Mundi, Polar Sun. If false: Every mythological correlation collapses.

2. Earth's orbital year length changed in discrete steps

Orbital period changed in quantized jumps — 225, 240, 260, 273, 360, 365.24 days — each triggered by electromagnetic close-encounters. Ancient cultures reformed calendars after each jump. If false: Sumerian king-list numbers are meaningless, Tzolkin is arbitrary, convergence of independent calendar reforms is coincidence.

3. The Solar System operates on electromagnetic, not purely gravitational, physics

Planets are charged bodies in Sun's plasma environment. Close approaches produce plasma discharges and orbital perturbations — same physics SAFIRE Project demonstrated at bell-jar scale. If false: No mechanism for orbital jumps, fossilisation events, or catastrophe record.

4. Ancient mythology is literal astronomical testimony

Gods, monsters, sky-battles, and creation events in every culture's texts are eyewitness accounts of real plasma events, not metaphors or psychology. If false: Primary source base is worthless as historical data.

5. Multiple catastrophic global events occurred within human record

World Flood (~3,147 BC), Venus encounters (~2,349 BC, ~2,193 BC, ~1,492 BC), Mars encounters (~776–686 BC), and 1774 CE petrification event are real physical events within human memory. If false: Catastrophes defining this project's chapter structure never happened on required timescale.

6. Conventional dating methods require recalibration

Radiocarbon, dendrochronology, and stratigraphy assume stable orbital environment. If Earth's orbit was shorter in antiquity — closer to Sun, higher solar flux — then C-14 production rates, tree-ring widths, and varve counts were all different. If false: Conventional dates stand uncorrected.

7. The conventional timeline has been artificially extended

Illig's Phantom Time (~297 years fabricated) is correct in substance. Broader Fomenko claim — Scaligerian chronology extends antiquity by centuries through phantom duplications — is also correct. See Other Challenges to Chronology. If false: Timeline synchronization is invalid.

8. Fossilisation is an electrical, rapid process

Evidence from Alberta power-line incident (E.R. Milton), Peter Mungo Jupp's research, and Thornhill's fulgurite demonstrations shows fossilisation occurs instantaneously under sufficient electrical discharge. SAFIRE Project confirmed plasma can transmute elements. If false: Deep time is real and fossil record is millions of years old.

How They Interlock

These are not independent — they form single structure:

• Proposition 3 (plasma physics) provides mechanism for 1, 2, and 5

• Proposition 1 (Saturn configuration) is origin point of 5's catastrophe sequence

• Proposition 2 (variable year) provides chronological key making 7 (phantom time) solvable

• Proposition 4 (mythology as testimony) provides source base for 5's dating

• Proposition 6 (dating recalibration) is methodological consequence of 2

• Proposition 8 (rapid fossilisation) is geological consequence of 3 and 5

Disprove mechanism (Proposition 3) and entire structure falls.

What We Do Not Claim

This project does not require specific theology, agreement on which mythological source is most accurate, literal correctness of any particular Fomenko chronology, or Young-Earth Creationism. It requires only that eight propositions survive empirical scrutiny.

We do not support radiocarbon or ice-core dating as reliable for pre–4077 BC chronology. We do not support ice ages before the Dark Ages — save for the south end of Mars during the Golden Age, which was indeed colder.

The Length of a Year Changes Throughout Antiquity

Earth's orbital radius — and year-length — changed with each electromagnetic close-encounter. Time-keeping began ~4,077 BC (225-day year); each jump drew Earth farther from the Sun, producing a slower orbit. The Sumerian King-List confirms the 225-day year arithmetically — reigns in tens of thousands of "years" dismissed as fantastical; Cook argues these record days; only a 225-day year resolves totals into whole-number multiples of 32 years.

Timeline Synchronization

Creation dates across civilizations split into two clusters (Septuagint ~5500 BCE vs. indigenous ~3100–4100 BCE). The gap is the fingerprint of redaction. Jno Cook's reconstruction shows different civilizations set year-zero at different chapters of the same sequence: 4077 BCE (Saturn nova), 3147 BCE (World Flood), 2349 BCE (Noah), 685 BCE (history begins). 00.03.03-cosmic-life-cycle.md
========================================================================

Cosmic Life Cycle

We are taught our Universe began with a Big Bang, yet every prediction of this theory has been refuted since the 1990s. Every civilization remembering the Golden Age described it starting with a "big bang" or incredible brightness. A Big Bang event indicates the moment of rebirth in each Solar System's life cycle, not the entire Universe.

Our Sun converts light gasses to heavy elements which fall to its interior and combine into a proto-planet with lightning scars and no life, orbiting from the inside. As it grows, energy charges it like a capacitor, forcing it to develop hollow spheres and a stronger magnetic field.

    A [video](https://www.nbcnews.com/id/wbna46725245) from 2012 of a proto-planet being *born* out of our Sun.

After becoming too big for stable internal orbit, the proto-planet is violently born from the Sun's surface (a coronal mass ejection). Its positively-charged EM field pushes it past the outer solar system toward the nearest negative surface charge — like Brown Dwarf Saturn. Once impregnated, the Dwarf's charge changes and it falls toward the nearest positive body — our Sun.

To become a planet, a proto-planet must be consumed by a larger host and carried back to Sun. Proto-Earth was consumed by Proto-Saturn and carried into Sun's orbit — our planet's conception. See Before Creation.

Unable to contain charging bodies within, the plasma sheath exploded, creating gas planets and pouring material into Sun's orbit. This coalesced around Birkeland currents forming moons and rock planets, forcing all into collinear configuration. This high-energy period is the Golden Age — when all life forms are created.

Planets grow; the collinear system breaks into a non-linear 'round-table' system marked by epic cataclysms spanning the Dark Ages (Old Testament). After instability resolves, planets violently break away to find final orbits. Modern history begins (New Testament).

After thousands of stable years, each planet loses density, drifts outward, eventually rips out of the solar system. The Sun's system empties, ready for another cycle.

Before the Golden Age, 3 Plasmoids lit up the Southern Hemisphere but were difficult to see above the equator.

Theoretical Context

This model — short cosmic life cycle at Solar System scale — is original synthesis from Saturnian Cosmology, Electric Universe theory, and ancient testimony. Wal Thornhill and EU framework are consistent, but Thornhill has not explicitly stated Solar Systems undergo birth-death-rebirth cycle.

Gap Theory (Ruin-Restitution)

Most aligned existing theory: indefinite interval between Genesis 1:1 and Genesis 1:2 — Hebrew tohu wa-bohu implies ruined state, not original one. Cosmos existed, was destroyed, was recreated: universe was already there before Genesis. Proponents: Thomas Chalmers (1814), G.H. Pember (1876), Scofield Reference Bible (1909).

Young Universe Creationism (YUC)

Interprets Genesis literally, placing cosmos at 6,000–10,000 years. While the cosmic life cycle model agrees current cycle may be young — consistent with Fomenko and Saturnian timelines — it does not require universe itself to be young.

01.00.00-before-creation.md
========================================================================

Before Creation

Ancient testimony from every civilization agrees: there was no history — no time — before "creation." All accounts describe the pre–Golden Age as lasting darkness. Saturn's dwarf system was receiving its first energy from the Sun, and with that energy came the first life forms capable of memory. As Saturn had not yet joined the Sun's orbit, there was no mechanism for reckoning time.

Saturn as a Brown Dwarf Star

According to Jno Cook's Recovering the Lost World (Chapter 2: A Synopsis), Earth was not always a planet of our Sun. From genesis, Earth was a satellite of Saturn — then a sub-brown dwarf star wandering through space. Saturn generated faint infrared light and plasma output, sustaining the Saturnian System far from solar influence. Venus, Neptune, Uranus, and Jupiter were not yet formed, nor were Mercury or Mars. All planets reached their final form through the electronic bond forming between our Sun and Brown Dwarf Saturn.

The Long Darkness: A Universal Memory

Cross-cultural mythology from every continent converges on one memory: before "creation," the sky was formless, dark, and chaotic. As Cook writes in Chapter 13: The Creation:

"It was universally held that the past before 'creation' was a long period during which the skies were in chaos and featureless turmoil."

Egypt, Mesopotamia, Mesoamerica, India, Scandinavia, and indigenous traditions worldwide corroborate this. The myths describe a real observational condition: Earth was enveloped inside Saturn's plasma coma in glow mode, obscuring all stars and the Sun. From the surface, nothing was visible but swirling luminous mist.

Dwardu Cardona's God Star (2006) traces these "chaos" descriptions across mythological traditions, noting the primordial condition was typically described as a hovering cloud or ocean of mist with a twisted vapour column reaching sky to Earth — consistent with plasma discharge in glow mode.

Life Awakens in the South

Nearly all life on Earth spawned during this "before creation" period — a very slow and gradual increase in energy as Saturn approached the Sun. The moment of creation is remembered by those who were created in it precisely because they were created much earlier.

In the southern hemisphere, a great source of light and power persisted throughout the before-creation time: the Peratt column — a plasma discharge stretching from Earth's South Pole into space, with three luminous ball plasmoids visible in the sky. This southern display (remembered as Hurakan, Muspelheim, the "three stones," and the turtle in Maya cosmology) provided such intense energy that it slowly awakened the sleepy, gradual evolution of each life form. It jumpstarted a period of rapid evolution, with humans reaching their final stable forms — organs, skin, and the bodies we recognize today — near the beginning of the Golden Age.

Those who witnessed "creation" in 4077 BC were not created in that moment. They had already evolved. What they witnessed was the start of time — the first moment that could be recorded.

Cook's Dates and the Problem of Timekeeping

Cook proposes a sequence of events before 4077 BC: Earth entering Saturn's coma (~10,900 BC), settling into sub-polar orbit (~8347 BC), and the Cosmic Egg becoming visible through thinning clouds (~4900 BC). His 10,900 BC figure comes from correlating with the "Younger Dryas" period in ice-core records — but we do not support radiocarbon or ice-core dating as reliable. Even if a cold period is recorded in ice, it is not clear when it happened, why, or how long it lasted from such methods alone. These events (coma, plasmoids, egg) are real memories and match mythological evidence, but the dates are unverifiable.

But Cook does not explain how such dates could have been calculated. Without a visible Sun, no solar calendar. Without stars, no sidereal reference. Without a timekeeping device, no way to count days or years. Even if Saturn or Earth had some rotation, Saturn's outer plasma shell would have made it impossible for any life on Earth to observe or record it. The coma obscured everything.

The first date that can be reliably recorded is 4077 BC. In that year, energy passing through the system forced the planets to rotate like electric engines. Arc mode ignited. The coma collapsed. The skies cleared. For the first time, there was something to count — a visible Sun, a daily cycle, a mechanism for reckoning time.

We therefore remove anything before 4077 BC from the timeline. The events (coma entry, plasmoids, sub-polar orbit, Cosmic Egg) are still real memories, but it is completely impossible to identify how much time passed between them. The "before creation" period is undatable.

Creation: 4077 BC

When Saturn nova'd in 4077 BC — transitioning from glow mode to arc mode and blazing suddenly like a sun — Earth's skies cleared for the first time. The Sun became visible. Regular solar time began. All founding myths, king lists, and calendrical "year one" dates cluster at or after this event. This is "Creation."

3 Plasmoids Southern Hemisphere

Another bright light source visible only from the Southern Hemisphere before the Golden Age is remembered as Hurakan in the Popol Vuh and Muspelheim in Norse cosmology.

The Peratt Column: Plasma Physics Meets Petroglyph Evidence

Anthony L. Peratt (Los Alamos National Laboratory) published landmark papers in IEEE Transactions on Plasma Science (2003, 2007) demonstrating that ~40% of millions of petroglyphs worldwide directly represent plasma column instabilities — a massive discharge column of streaming electrons stretching from Earth's South Pole into space.

Structure of the Column

According to Peratt's reconstruction (extended by Cook in Chapter 10):

• A plasma column in arc mode stretched from Earth's South Pole outward into space

• Bent ~30 degrees off Earth's rotational axis (visible from as far north as ~70°N)

• Three ball-shaped plasmoids formed at ~165,000, ~190,000, and ~435,000 miles from Earth

• Current consisted of 56 Birkeland filaments converging to 28 then twisting into 4

• Plasma in synchrotron emission producing visible light, UV, X-rays, and radio frequencies

The three plasmoids correspond to the three stones and turtle of Maya cosmology, and the three mounds encoded in burial construction worldwide. Cook proposes a duration of ~2,550 years for the display, but as discussed in Before Creation, no timekeeping was possible before 4077 BC — these dates are unverifiable.

Petroglyph Evidence

Peratt's team analysed over 4,000,000 petroglyphs. Key findings:

• Carved high on cliff faces with unobstructed southern views

• Many carved "two or three times" — humans returning to re-record after temporary disappearance

• Three epochs of concentrated worldwide activity matching three "showings" in Mesoamerican sources

• The squatting man / standing humanoid form (found from Australia to Peru to Africa to Europe) reproduces the Z-pinch instability shape in high-current plasma columns

• The Nazca Lines are ground-level representations of the north-south electron stream visible overhead

Why the Column Was in the South

Earth was inside Saturn's lower plasmasphere, below Saturn's equatorial plane. Massive electron flows streamed northward toward Saturn, using Earth as part of the circuit.

01.02.00-spawning-of-the-first-life-forms.md

Spawning of the First Life Forms

The memory of 'creation' shared by mythologies was witnessed by life forms that had already spawned and could remember it, recording it later during the Golden Age. The Younger Edda states:

And when the heated blasts from Muspelheim met the rime [of the rivers], so that it melted into drops, then, by the might of him who sent the heat, the drops quickened into life and took the likeness of a man, who got the name Ymer.

Every mythology states that powerful forces in the sky spawned all life forms after its own image — which changed many times. As the Golden Age approached, the three southern plasmoids cycled through different shapes, generating different energy patterns until they formed the First Man pattern. This ideal pattern provided the scalar blueprint for spawning the first humans.

The first life forms — Golems — were not shaped from clay but condensed from a dense, mercury-like liquid metal by the sustained scalar field of the Saturnian system. See Scalar Energy as the basis of Creation for the detailed mechanism.

The First Man Pattern: Plasmoid as Blueprint

As the Peratt column (before 4077 BC, undatable) wound down, the three southern ball plasmoids cycled through morphological forms including the squatting man figure that Peratt's analysis of 4,000,000 petroglyphs identified as the most widely reproduced image in human prehistory.

This is the "First Man" encoded in myth — not an ancestor, but the plasma template from which humans were physically formed:

• Hebrew: Adam Kadmon — primordial cosmic human; the Golem tradition preserves the memory of beings shaped from base matter and animated by divine force

• Hindu: Purusha — cosmic man from whose dismembered body the universe was constructed

• Vedic/Mesoamerican: squatting deity of lightning and sky (Huracan / God K)

• Norse: Ymer — born of Muspelheim's heat meeting rime; "drops quickening into life" describes liquid metallic substance animated by plasma energy

The Edda directly encodes the plasma mechanism: heat from the southern column ionizes cold primordial waters, and from charged plasma drops life quickens. This is not allegory but description of an observed sky event preserved for millennia.

Homo sapiens and the Upper Paleolithic Revolution

The abruptness of the Upper Paleolithic transition is consistent with an external speciation event rather than internal evolutionary pressure. Cook summarises:

"We, Homo sapiens, did not slowly evolve from these hominids. When we do finally appear, it is complete and with astounding suddenness — and with hundreds of specific stone tools, spear throwers, detachable harpoons, serrated knives, thread cutters, basketry, weaving, beads, and cosmetics."
— Jno Cook, Chapter 7: Ice Ages and Humans

Cook's chapter links this to ice-age chronology; this timeline does not support ice ages before the Dark Ages (save the south end of Mars during the Golden Age).

The "out of Africa" hypothesis cannot account for the simultaneous global appearance of the same material culture package. A plasma speciation event acting on dispersed H.erectus populations produces the observed pattern: multiple origin points, rapid spread, and immediate cultural explosion.

Scalar Energy as the basis of Creation

Scalar Energy (ZPE) physics explains how original species were created in a high-energy environment without evolution. Large electromagnetic fields cause quantum fluctuation reorganizing matter. As plasmoids in the southern sky took new energetic forms, quantum fluctuations on Earth forced similar patterns within a dense, mercury-like liquid metal on the early surface. This conductive substance allowed scalar-driven biogenesis: quantum fluctuations restructured it, shaping the first Golems. Early Golems had no organs (they didn't need to eat, see, or move). Over the Golden Age, sustained scalar energy forced internal differentiation — rudimentary organs forming as metal transmuted into biological tissue. Not Darwinian evolution, but continued external scalar influence.

The Electric Environment

The scalar field for spontaneous biogenesis was produced by the Saturnian system's sustained plasma engagement. Jno Cook's chronology proposes a sequence of phases before 4077 BC, but as discussed in Before Creation, no timekeeping was possible — these phases are real memories, undatable:

Before 4077 BC (undatable): Direct electromagnetic modulation by Saturn. Earth possessed equatorial rings — the Absu — suspended up to 8,500 miles above the equator. See The Absu. Combined with Saturn's plasma column, Earth was inside a double-layered electromagnetic furnace. Saturn eventually became visible as "the Egg" through thinning plasma (Finnish Kalevala: "Ilmatar drifts upon the waters for 700 years" — the duration is mythological, not verifiable).

4077 BC (Nova Event): Saturn transitioned from glow to arc mode. The coma collapsed instantly, generating peak scalar discontinuity: "Saturn burst into arc mode, threw out enormous matter, developed a corona, and lit up like a sun."

The Sustained Scalar Field: 4077–3147 BC

Following the nova, Earth entered 1,000 years of continuous arc-mode plasma — the Era of the Gods. Cook documents: "Mankind was subjected for a thousand years to a varying magnetic field, a by-product of the rotating Birkeland current." This oscillating field was not merely therapeutic but generative, reorganizing matter at the molecular level.

Mars descended the plasma column repeatedly, each visit accompanied by massive arcing to Earth's surface.

01.03.00-all-planets-and-suns-are-hollow.md
========================================================================

All planets and suns are hollow

The reason for the Absu having multiple layers is described by Electromagnetism. Any object with energy passing through it will polarize, causing magnetic flux lines to appear in layers. At high amplitude, matter sticks to each layer, forming concentric circles that increasingly capacitate energy. Charging a space body with energy causes it to grow; as it grows, the gap between inner circles expands, causing the planet to become hollow.

The Lazeria Map Collection: Evidence for Polar Opening

Watch: Lazeria Map Collection — Is the Earth Hollow?

From the 1500s–1700s, independent European cartographers recorded the same Arctic details: four rivers flowing inward toward a central sea, draining into Earth's interior. These were navigational maps, not allegorical works.

Gerard Mercator (1569) produced the most famous world map showing the North Pole as four large Bargo Islands separated by four rivers flowing northward to a central polar sea. Latin text: "The rivers flow to the north and empty into the inner earth. The canyon beneath the pole is approximately 33 leagues wide." His grandson's versions (1587–1636) retain identical detail — a transmitted 70-year tradition.

Matthias Quad (1600) confirmed: "The large island has at its center a sea, which in its center lies the Great Dark Canyon." He was banned by the Church that year.

Nearly every major navigational map 1570–1650 shows Frisland between Greenland and Iceland — a large island later declared "mythological." Soundings as late as 1944 confirmed a submerged landmass ~27 miles diameter, ~30 fathoms deep.

Arctic Explorer Accounts

Real explorer accounts consistently report conditions impossible on solid ice:

• Joseph Moxon (1652): Dutch sailor reached 2° beyond the North Pole — "as warm as Amsterdam in summer"

• Elisha Kent Kane (1854): At 82°N found "vast open polar seas with heavy breakers," plus butterflies, bees, wolves, bears, geese

• Dr. Fridtjof Nansen: Recorded it becoming warmer approaching the pole; compass erratic; open water northwest; turned back due to compass failure, not ice

• Multiple explorers: Animals migrate north, not south, in winter — inexplicable if the pole is only frozen ocean

The wreckage of USS Jeannette (crushed 1881) drifted 3,500 miles through the presumed polar ice cap — mechanically impossible without a through-channel.

The Golden Age

The Garden of Earthly Delights by Hieronymus Bosch depicting the beginning, middle, and end of the Golden Age.

This period of stable collinear configuration is remembered throughout the civilizations of the Earth as the Golden
Age. The Hindu Brahma, Yama, Vishnu, and Manu describe the age of the “Brilliant Yima”.
To the Chinese, it was the reign of “Yellow Emperor” Huang-ti, founder of Taoism.
In Northern Europe, Peace of Frodi.
In the Christian Bible, this 930-year period was the Garden of Eden.
The Caribs of Surinam recall the lost epoch: "In a time long past, so long past that even the grandmothers of our grandmothers were not yet born, the world was quite other than what it is today: the trees were forever in fruit; the animals lived in perfect harmony, and the little agouti played fearlessly with the beard of the jaguar." David Talbott quotes this in "The Myth of the Golden Age" (1997); it also appears in Remembering the End of the World (documentary).

"The world was quite other than what it is today: the trees were forever in fruit;
the animals lived in perfect harmony, and the little agouti played fearlessly with the beard of the jaguar."
— Caribs of Surinam, quoted in Talbott, "The Myth of the Golden Age" (1997)

"In the elaborated memories of the Golden Age or ancestral paradise, there is no sacrifice, no war, no sickness or death, no division of nation against nation, and no division of language between man and animal, or between man and man. And thus, no need for ritual cleansing or defense. Whatever the natural conditions may have been during this celebrated epoch, they were sufficient to plant in collective memory a root metaphor for benevolent creation, cosmic harmony, and the unity of life."
— ifiseeu.com, Sacrifice & Amnesia

The cosmic mountain appears under many names: Meru (Hindu), Hera-Berezaiti (Iranian), Kwen-Lun (Chinese), Colhuacan (Mexican), Olympus and Zion (Talbott, The Saturn Myth).

As each planet was connected in-series,
their axial tilt would stay synchronized even as the entire system Orbited the Sun, but after the planets left the

and their axial tilts would be fixed indefinitely. This is the reason Earth, Mars, Saturn, and Neptune have
matching axial tilts within 3 degrees. By the end of
this period, each planet in the configuration had become a "fully charged" capacitor, unable to accept more charge or
grow larger. The configuration violently came to an end, and the age of cataclysm began.

The Seven Days of Creation: A Compressed Memory of the Saturnian Epoch

The Genesis creation account is literally true but compressed. Each "day" is a cosmological epoch lasting hundreds of years, bookended by planetary climaxes. Day 1 = 4077 BC (Saturn arc-mode ignition); Day 7 = 3147 BC (Golden Age collapse). For the full mapping: Seven Days of Creation in the Appendix.

The 225-Day Year

Evidence from the Sumerian King-List

Saturn was called Kronos, the God before time began. Before clocks and calendars, planetary positions were the only way to measure time. When Saturn's system joined the Sun's orbit, time began. Our current Sun was still hidden behind Saturn (and Jupiter). Earth's spin remained constant — a "day" stayed the same — but the year was only 225 days: 40% faster than today's 365.25-day orbit. Year-lengths shifted periodically as Earth jumped between orbits, each jump drawing less solar energy and producing a slower orbit. Saturn or Kronos translates to "time." The visible crescent of Saturn — lit by the real Sun at Saturn's rim — was the first clock. Earth's rotation made it appear to turn; Talbott's revolving-crescent article details the four-phase daily cycle. When Jupiter displaced Saturn after 3147 BC, Jupiter replaced "the older God of Time and Beginnings." Sumerian King-List (List of Kings Before the Flood) — reigns in tens of thousands of "years" dismissed as fantastical. Cook: these record days; only a 225-day year resolves totals into whole-number multiples of 32 years. Grand total: 241,200 days = 1,072 years. A 225-day year = 0.72 AU (Venus's orbit); Kepler's third law gives ~214 days for 0.70 AU — consistent. Full tables: Year-Length and Calendar Evidence.

Saturn Collinear Configuration (Tree of Life)

Until the moment of 'creation', Earth had been hiding like a seedling safely inside Saturn's nurturing brown-dwarf system. Birkeland currents forced the planets into a new collinear configuration, connecting each planet from north to south pole, like batteries. In The Saturn Myth David Talbott writes:

"Accounts of Saturn's appearance suggest that the planet hung ominously close to the earth. In early ritual and astronomy Saturn appears as the primeval sun, described as a figure of terrifying splendour. Today, Saturn appears as a bare speck of light following the same visual path as the solar orb. But during the legendary Golden Age, Saturn stood in the north. Legends from every continent depict the primeval sun as an immense, fiery globe at the north celestial pole—the visual pivot of the heavens."

The cone shape appears in the Egyptian White Crown and Persian Crown of Mithra. See Atlantis and the Tree of Knowledge.

The cosmic mountain celebrated around the world, seen as a pillar of fire and light rising along the world axis; as a radiant city or temple of heaven, as the prototype for any sacred space on earth.

02.02.00-earth-started-rotating-visibly.md

Earth started rotating visibly

During the collinear configuration, planets in the northern chain (Mars, Venus, Saturn, Jupiter) were aligned pole-to-pole and did not spin independently. Earth's orientation was fixed toward Saturn. In The Saturn Myth David Talbott describes Saturn as fixed at the north celestial pole—the visual pivot of the heavens.

Jno Cook describes the moment the crescent became the day/night marker:

"As the Earth rotated (below Saturn), the crescent appeared to move counterclockwise around the outer rim of the globe on a daily basis. The crescent dimmed as it moved up toward the 'top' of Saturn during the Earth's daylight hours, and brightened again as it dipped down to the bottom at night. The first experience of clock-time had started for humanity."
— Jno Cook, saturniancosmology.org/time.php

Whether Earth began rotating then or had always rotated and 4077 marks when the crescent became the day/night marker is ambiguous. Talbott does not assign dates.

Absu layers surrounded Planets

Visible on Earth's Southern Hemisphere was "the Absu", also called "The Abyss" or Firmament — outer layers of Saturn's plasma sheath appearing as obscuring equatorial rings, a cosmic 'sea' in the southern sky. Jno Cook describes it:

What the Absu Was

Earth possessed concentric equatorial plasma rings — analogous to Saturn's rings today — during the polar configuration. From Chapter 12: "People in the northern hemisphere would have seen the rings in the southern skies at night... standing 40–50 degrees up, arcing from east to west. At night the Absu was brilliant — lighted directly by the Sun."

The rings persisted until 2349 BC, when they fell — the Flood of Noah.

Northern Configuration

From anywhere on the northern hemisphere of the Earth, one could look northwards and see in order: Mars, Venus, then the gas giant Saturn. Behind Saturn was the gas giant Jupiter, which was not yet visible. Behind Jupiter, at a 26.7 degree angle, was our Sun. All together, they form an unmistakable Star and crescent in the sky worshiped throughout the Persian, Roman, Byzantine, and Ottoman Empires, and throughout Islam today. This deity was known in Egypt as Osiris the Triform God, and as The Trinity in Babylonian and Abrahamic religion.

In The Saturn Myth, David Talbott writes:

"Accounts of Saturn's appearance suggest that the planet hung ominously close to the earth. In early ritual and astronomy Saturn appears as the primeval sun, described as a figure of terrifying splendour. But during the legendary Golden Age, Saturn stood in the north. Legends from every continent depict the primeval sun as an immense, fiery globe at the north celestial pole—the visual pivot of the heavens."
— David Talbott, The Saturn Myth (1980)

The polar column (World Mountain, axis mundi) stretched along the configuration axis—a stream or jet of material between planets. Talbott: legends say the creator ruled from the mountaintop, where stood the original paradise with four life-bearing streams.

was the Midgard Dragon / Serpent / Doomsday Comet
and appeared stretched out in many shapes and colors, none of which resembled a planet. It is during this time that Mars

Southern Configuration

In 2003, Anthony L. Peratt described ancient testimony of 3 intense "plasmoids" in the southern region of the world. The 3 plasmoids were Mercury, Neptune, and Uranus forming the lower end of the conjunction. Snorre's Edda states: "Still there was before a world to the south which was called Muspelheim. It is light and hot, and so bright and dazzling that no stranger, who is not a native there, can stand it." The fiery apparition of the Squatter Man figure in the south was recorded as the Phoenix, Burning Man, Wicker Man, and Quetzalcoatl.

Atlantis and the Tree of Knowledge

Atlantis was the first holy city — First Jerusalem — built on the north poles of Mars and Earth to harness the energy of the Collinear Configuration.

The planets in the northern configuration created a Tree of Life display directly above Atlantis and its three cities Og, Aryan, and the 'Emerald City' of Poseida. Poseida, the capital, existed as a massive pyramid built from grown emeralds — maximizing conductivity of cosmic energy. Its rulers jealously guarded their inventions until the city became known as the Tree of Knowledge. After the fall, these elites escaped with their knowledge, founding the Pyramidal Empire.

Plato's Description Cannot Be Located on Earth

TODO: deep dive into plato text

The only ancient account comes from Plato's Timaeus and Critias (c. 360 BCE). Read literally, his description is geographically impossible for present Earth — but consistent with a city at the north pole of Mars.

"Larger than Libya and Asia combined" (Timaeus 24e–25a). No terrestrial continent satisfies this. In the Saturnian framework, Mars was nearly as large as Earth and accessible from Earth's north pole via the plasma column.

"Ships and voyages were not yet" (Critias 113e–114a). If Atlantis couldn't be reached by ship, it wasn't mid-Atlantic. Plato describes a place reached by the Bifröst — the natural plasma bridge along the planetary column.

Concentric rings "sheltered from the north" (Critias 113d–116a). A city around a north pole with the continent falling southward matches the Martian north polar cap. Orichalcum, a metal "dug out in many parts of the island," has never been identified on Earth — consistent with Martian surface mineralogy.

Repeated catastrophes (Critias 111–112). Egyptian priests confirmed cyclical destruction: Atlantis was destroyed during Saturn-system collapse (c. 3147 BCE) and the Flood accompanying Mars' orbital departure.

The First Jews of Atlantis (First Jerusalem)

The term 'Jew' originally referred to anyone worshiping Saturn as Creator God (YHWH, El, Elohim, Ba'al). The priest class claimed authority over all aspects of life, forming the protective outer layer of the Pyramidal Empire throughout history.

By keeping strict lineage separation, they passed down inherited authority over centuries. They appear to have no origin because their first appearance was before Atlantis's fall, when they exchanged advanced technology for obedience. Abrahamic religions remember this as the Garden of Eden and the Tree of Knowledge.

Open Investigation: The City Names Og, Aryan, and Poseida

These names do not appear in Plato. They make structural sense but their earliest traceable appearances are in 19th–21st century non-canonical sources. Investigation ongoing.

Sources traced: Og: Book of Giants (DSS 4Q531). Aryan: Blavatsky, The Secret Doctrine (1888). Poseida: Edgar Cayce A.R.E. readings, 364-series (1923–1944).

Interplanetary Entanglement

During Golden Age, high-amplitude, low-voltage energy flowed through each planet in the configuration Norse called "The Bifröst." As planets did not spin, each pole acted as strong quantum entanglement between worlds. Building pyramidal structures at these locations enabled humans to travel instantaneously through the Bifröst. Egyptian mythology's "Boat of Ra" — which transported Ra between planets — looks strikingly like modern wormhole depiction:

The Plasma Column as Trans-Planetary Bridge

Saturnian Cosmology identifies the mechanism: sustained plasma stream in arc mode connecting Saturn's polar axis to Earth's north magnetic pole — visible, luminous, electrically-active column. Different cultures recorded it under different names, all describing same phenomenon.

Bifröst — The Norse Bridge

Norse Eddas describe plasma stream as bridge Bifrost between Earth (Midgard) and "Other World" (Asgard). From north Atlantic, stream appeared as arch spanning heavens from southwest to Saturn overhead. Norse legends also describe it as inverted tree with roots at top — exactly what plasma column breaking into streamers would look like. This is World Tree (Yggdrasil) and Axis Mundi of dozens of cultures.

The Rainbow — A Misidentified Plasma Plume

"Rainbow as divine covenant" (Genesis/Noah, Enuma Elish) is mistranslation. In Enuma Elish, Marduk (Jupiter) plants "war bow" in sky after flood — south polar plasma plume standing vertically, not horizontal arc of refracted sunlight. Sign given to Noah was likely reappearance of polar plasma plumes after orbital disruption.

Rainbow Serpent — Polar Plume as Sky-Arch

Australian Aboriginal Rainbow Serpent and North American Great Horned Serpent are identified with north polar plasma plume, appearing each time Earth's orbit shifted. Serpent-form (luminous arch) combined with auroral colours produced universal "rainbow serpent" archetype across unconnected cultures.

Boat of Ra — Saturn's Plasma Crescent

Talbott identifies Egyptian Boat of Ra as visual appearance of crescent of plasma light revolving around Saturn — not Sun. Egyptian descriptions have Boat sailing through sky at night and journeying through underworld during day — opposite of solar behaviour — confirming it tracked Saturn. Dead traveled Boat of Ra to reach Osiris: polar plasma bridge as literal passage between worlds.

Eye of Ra — The Polar Alignment

Eye of Ra (Wedjat) records polar alignment seen from Earth: Saturn as eyeball, Mercury as iris, Mars as pupil, Saturn's rings forming almond-shaped lids, plasma stream extending downward as Eye's tail. Uraeus (cobra) on pharaoh's crown is same symbol — plasma stream from Saturn's pole descending to Earth.

The Golden Age Ends Violently.

Within a very short time, all 9 planets (including Earth) break from Saturn's configuration and exit the outer layers of Saturn's protective plasma sheath. The planets, having left the collinear configuration, violently competed for position in a new orbit — forming an in-parallel circular non-linear configuration orbiting the Sun for the next 1655 years. Humanity encounters death as it enters a period of extended darkness, famine, pestilence, and confusion.

Earth's Orbit Changes: Year Extends to 240 Days

The Maya calendar starts with Earth jumping into its second orbit under Jupiter's influence on 3114 B.C.E. with each year recorded as 240 days long. This was the first time humanity glimpsed our Sun and gas-giant Jupiter, hidden behind Saturn until now. This new Sun proved too bright; life on earth escaped into dark caves. Occasionally Jupiter orbited in front of the Sun, appearing together as a radiant throne in the sky.

Note on dates: The collinear configuration breakup is dated to 3147 BCE. The Mayan Long Count begins 33 years later at 3114 BCE, marking Earth's settlement into Jupiter's new orbit. Both dates are correct — they record different moments of the same transition.

And the heavens departed as a scroll when it is rolled together; and every mountain and island were moved out of their places... And said to the mountains and rocks, Fall on us, and hide us from the face of him that sitteth on the throne, and from the wrath of the Lamb.
—Revelation 6:12-17

The First Great Deluge

As Earth passed through Saturn's outer plasma sheath (The Absu), the layer cooled into sodium-salt water, becoming heavy and crashing into the planet surface — causing the first great deluge recorded by each civilization. Earth received its salty oceans from Saturn, which is why the Atlantic is the Sea of Kronos. The NOAA cannot legitimately answer: Why is the ocean salty, but rivers flowing into it are not?

Many great deluges have taken place during the [thousands of] years since Athens and Atlantis were preeminent... The floods, especially the third great flood before Deucalion, washed away most of Athens' fertile soil.
-Plato's Critias 111-112

When the Sun Age came, there had passed 400 years. Then came 200 years, then 76. Then all mankind was lost and drowned and turned to fishes.
-Aztec Codex Chimalpopoca

This was the first deluge. A second deluge would follow when Jupiter's Absu collapsed at 2349 BCE.

Jupiter replaces Saturn as the new Saviour (aka Zeus, Thor, King Arthur)

2860 BCE — All planets enter a stable Nonlinear Orbit

Jupiter's benevolent reign was a model of the "Good King" that inspired religions worldwide. Each new saviour planet carried a dual nature: first as liberator, then — as it too caused catastrophe — becoming the new archetype of evil. Jupiter was no exception.

For the full chain: Saturn → Jupiter → Venus → Mars → The Sun

Jupiter the Saviour: The Good King

Resolving electric imbalances between planets, Jupiter became King of the planets and reigned over a 511-year stability period. King Jupiter sat upon the Cosmic Mountain — Mount Olympus, Parnassus, Sinai. This "mountain" was Jupiter's South Pole plasma coma tail. Jupiter-the-Saviour appeared across cultures as just and benevolent ruler: Zeus (Greek king of gods), Odin/Thor (Norse storm-bringer), Marduk (Babylonian dragon-slayer), Indra (Hindu warrior-king who slew Vritra), The Dagda (Celtic "Good God").

Jupiter the Destroyer: The Jealous Tyrant

The same myths record Jupiter's jealousy, rage, and eventual fall. Zeus devoured wife Metis whole — swallowing wisdom to prevent prophecy. He wielded thunderbolts against his own children.

This is accurate observational recording. The plasma discharges from Jupiter's south pole that first brought order were — as Jupiter grew unstable — the same forces causing destruction. At approximately 2349 BCE, Jupiter disappeared from position and consumed Venus — drawing it into close encounter, causing comet-mode and the Ten Plagues.

Jupiter swallowing Venus: Zeus swallowing Metis (giving "birth" to Athena/Venus from his brow); the Burning Bush at Sinai (Jupiter's plasma discharge); Thor's battle with the Midgard Serpent at Ragnarok.

Jupiter's burning was interpreted as divine signal. As documented in Jupiter catches fire, the coma ignited. The jealous, angry God of the Old Testament — rebranding Jupiter's physical violence into theological command.

"The LORD your God, who is among you, is a jealous God…"
-Deuteronomy 6:14-16

The word jealous is the tell. A purely theological inventor would not describe an invisible God as jealous of visible things. Only a real physical entity — a planet whose worshippers could observe its decline and be seduced by newer, brighter competitors — required the threat: do not follow other gods.

The Fall of the Round Table

The nonlinear orbit grew unstable, resulting in the Breaking of the Round Table. King Arthur "died" — Jupiter ceased to dominate. The quest for the Holy Grail became humanity's unfulfilled longing. 03.04.03-pyramids-myths-vs-reality.md

Pyramids: Myths vs Reality

During the Dark Ages, humans exploited powerful natural Birkeland currents coursing between planets in Jupiter's orbit. They built pyramids at intersections of electromagnetic ley lines — an "Artificial Bifröst" making interplanetary travel possible. All pyramids are man-made quantum tunnels built at locations of concentrated cosmic energy between planets.

Mainstream claims about the pyramids are debunked by evidence Egyptologists ignore. Tombs: No mummy ever found; King's Chamber "sarcophagus" is unmarked; Al-Ma'mun found coins, not a body. Slaves: No depictions of block-dragging; no ramp remnants; Davidovich proved blocks are geopolymeric concrete cast in moulds. Bronze Age: Steel tools embedded in Khufu masonry; Herodotus records iron; hack-saw marks require steel. Diorite vessels: Cast, not carved; Nefertiti head has mould seam.

Full evidence: Pyramids: Myths Evidence Table. For why mainstream dates the Maya "collapse" to 1000+ years ago — denying Horde construction and recent genocide — see Common Questions: Maya civilization.

What Were Pyramids Actually For?

Pyramids are found not just in Egypt but on every continent — across Mesoamerica (Maya, Aztec), China, Sudan, Bosnia, Indonesia, and the Pacific. They cluster along the equatorial band and at locations of concentrated electromagnetic energy. This global distribution cannot be explained by Fomenko's theory that they were Horde imperial monuments or Christian Easter-cake symbols — that does not account for pyramids predating Christianity and spanning cultures that had no contact with the Rus.

Pyramids were Portals

The Saturnian Cosmology model provides the missing context: during the Dark Ages, enormous electrical discharges — Birkeland currents — coursed between planets in close orbital proximity. Pyramids were built at intersections of these electromagnetic ley lines as cathodic protection structures — essentially Faraday cages designed to channel and ground catastrophic cosmic electrical energy, protecting the structures and their occupants from the plasma discharges devastating the planet's surface.

This is the same principle behind church and cathedral architecture — spires, metal roofing, placement on ancient ley lines — which are the later, smaller descendants of pyramid technology. Pyramids were active during the Dark Ages when the cosmic electrical environment was intense. They are inactive today because the Solar System stabilized and the Birkeland currents between planets ceased.

The construction techniques — geopolymer concrete, megalithic precision — were not invented during the Dark Ages. They were inherited from the Golden Age, carried forward by survivors of the cataclysm that ended it. The knowledge was applied out of desperate necessity, not imperial vanity.

Wuxing and the Five Planets — Five Elements as Observational Record

Towards the end of the Dark Ages, Earth shared the sky with four other bodies in a visible, nonlinear configuration: Venus, Mars, Jupiter, and Mercury — five planets circling and balancing each other, exchanging discharges and tidal effects. This timeline argues that Wuxing (五行, the classical "Five Phases" or "Five Elements") was developed in that period not as philosophy, religion, or martial art, but as the observational record of that configuration. The five elements were not speculative; they were what observers saw and whose causality they acknowledged.

Five Planets, Five Elements

Each of the five bodies displayed a dominant element to terrestrial witnesses:

• Mercury — Metal. The planet most associated with metal; its appearance and role in electric and metallurgical lore (e.g. Hermes, Thoth, alchemy) align with a "metal" signature.

• Mars — Fire. Although Mars also carried metal (e.g. iron shell, asteroid belt), fire was central — the red glow, plasma, and Prometheus-as-fire-giver.

• Venus — Water. Not because Venus was "made of" water, but because it was classified as the most liquid — fluid, flowing, comet-like; the element of flow and transformation.

• Jupiter — Wood (木). In this reading, wood stands for electricity, growth, and lightning — the great storm god, the king of the gods, the source of bolts and life-giving discharge.

• Earth — Earth (土). The ground underfoot; the stable centre from which the other four were observed.

Between the planets, observers could see interactions: arcs of lightning, plasma tails, and electromagnetic balance. The cycles of Wuxing (generating, controlling) map onto the cycles of how these five bodies acted on one another. At the time, all of this science was visible in the sky and could not be suppressed.

Planets as combinations. The planets were regarded as the elements themselves — not as "pure" fire or water. Each planet was a combination of all elements, with one element as the head. Wuxing asserts nothing about pure fire or water doing this or that; it asserts that these planets did things, and that the interactions between planets gave clues about the lower elements (fire, water, earth, metal, wood). The real nature of Wuxing is combination with one element at the head.

Chaos, Meteorology, and Respect for All Gods

The late Dark Ages were a time of great chaos and meteorology — interplanetary lightning, year-length changes, and successive "saviour" planets that became destroyers. No human could know which god would next appear to strike them down or to intervene. The one who had departed (Saturn, then Jupiter, then Venus in turn) remained in ritual and memory. Wuxing, in this reading, is the history of planetary configurations between the Golden Age and the current age: a record of which bodies were in the sky and how they balanced and opposed each other — and a reminder to respect all of them, including the one who had left.

Not Philosophy, Religion, or Martial Art

Mainstream scholarship often treats Wuxing as a Warring States–era philosophy or a framework for medicine, statecraft, and martial arts. This timeline does not deny later uses but proposes an earlier, observational origin: Wuxing began as the codification of what was seen when five planets shared the sky. Philosophy, religion, and martial application came later, building on a cosmology that was once direct observation.

Full investigation: Wuxing — Five Planets, Five Elements.

— Jno Cook, Saturnian Cosmology

The Chain Closes

Each successive planet earned the title of saviour, then revealed itself as another source of destruction. The irony: every time they installed a new jealous god, its successor exposed the previous one as fraud. Their strategy — claiming each saviour as Saturn's legitimate heir — guaranteed eventual exposure.

05.03.00-deep-state-centralize-world.md
========================================================================

Deep State Centralizes World Religion

After roughly 822 years in exile, the Deep State resurfaced to rebuild their Monotheistic slave empire and claim sole authority to speak for the Creator God. They built a new headquarters at the narrowest part of the Bosphorus in north-west Istanbul — strategically advantageous. This metropolis was called Yoros in Turkish, Hierosolyma in Latin, and Kiev / Tsar-Grad (City of the Czar) in Cyrillic. In redacted Greek Mythology it was remembered as Troia or Troy. This was the holiest city on Earth because it was modelled after the Heavenly Jerusalem from the holy texts. This model (6 gates and 3 walls) was first seen as hexagons surrounding brown-dwarf Saturn in the Golden Age and is still seen today. In old versions of Jerusalem each of the 3 walls had 6 gates, yielding 6-6-6 associated with Satan i.e. Saturn.

Why Rebuild a Celestial City on Earth?

By the 11th century, planetary saviours were gone. The Sun was the new god: you don't need priests anymore. People were free — and knew it. (See New Religion of Constancy.)

No free civilization would have chosen to rebuild a replica of the old celestial slave-city. The 3 walls and 6 gates encoded Saturn's hexagonal signature. David Talbott confirms the creation myth's central subject was "the construction of a celestial dwelling — a cosmic city, temple, or kingdom — revered across the millennia as the prototype of sacred space" (see: origins of myth). Every earthly sacred city was a copy of this original.

The only group needing to recreate that geometry was a priestly class deriving authority from it. The symbolic recreation was an attempt to reassert control over a humanity that had just freed itself. Jerusalem was the answer: we will rebuild the house of God on Earth, and you will need us to enter it.

Open Investigation: Mars/Atlantis as Architectural Template

Whether the builders consciously replicated the concentric-ring geometry of Atlantis on Mars — Plato's alternating rings of water and land (Critias, 113e–114a) — or worked from Saturn's hexagonal pattern alone, remains open. The connection is inferential: Saturn's hexagon provides 6-fold symmetry; Atlantis's rings provide the walled-enclosure template; both were simultaneously visible during the Golden Age; the Deep State originated in Atlantis and would have carried both memories.

The Fixed Calendar and the New Faith

The fixed calendar provided the Deep State with a faith system based on constancy. Jerusalem #1 in Istanbul was renamed Constantinople and became headquarters of the New Religion of Constancy. Since all information on the old Gods was kept secret in Jerusalem archives, common people gradually forgot them. Stories fell into mythology, replaced by accounts of the Sun traversing seasons, later becoming the farmer's almanac.

The Crucifixion: Proof of Intent

What confirms Jerusalem as a Deep State control centre is what happened there later. The Crucifixion was not piety but political suppression: public execution of a popular figure who threatened the priestly monopoly, at the very site built to reassert that monopoly. A genuine holy city does not execute its prophets. A control centre does — when the prophet's message threatens the control.

05.03.01-new-religion-of-constancy.md
========================================================================

New Religion of Constancy

The idea that things have always been the way they are — that the Sun has always shone as it does today, that the sky has always looked this way — is the foundational lie of the age we live in. The very names Constantinople and Constantine carry this lie in their root: Latin constans, meaning "standing firm, unchanging, constant."

The Doctrine of Constancy

When Deep State rebuilt Jerusalem at Istanbul, they constructed a worldview. The fixed calendar was part of a broader campaign: uniformitarianism — belief that present processes always operated at same rates. The Sun rises. It sets. It always did this. There were never planet-gods. Never a Golden Age. The cosmos is, and has always been, constant.

Constantine and Constantinople: Names Are the Program

Constantine derives from Latin constans (steadfast, unchanging): "the one who stands unchanged." Constantinople = "City of the Unchanging One."

The name was chosen for this purpose. In a tradition where titles were transferred to match the current theological programme, it would be remarkable if the most important city in the new Sun religion received its name by accident.

Constantine worshipped Sol Invictus — Unconquered Sun — before his supposed conversion. Sol Invictus was itself an assertion of constancy: a sun that cannot be conquered, stands forever. Transition to Christian Sun-Christ was rebranding, not conversion.

Why Constancy Was Necessary

In the centuries after Mars departed, living memory of planet-gods was fading but had not disappeared. If people remembered the chain of saviours — Saturn replaced by Jupiter, Jupiter by Venus, Venus by Mars, Mars by Sun — they would expect the Sun also to be replaced, and its priesthood with it.

Solution: deny the chain entirely. The Sun was not the latest in a succession — it was eternal. The cosmos did not change. And the priesthood administering this eternal order was itself eternal, stretching back into fabricated past.

The Constancy Program

• The Fixed Calendar — Anchoring time to solar cycles and projecting backward created artificial constancy, eventually pushed through hundreds of thousands of years.

• Suppression of Catastrophism — Every memory of planetary encounters and plasma cataclysms redacted into myth, allegory, or madness.

• Uniformitarianism (19th century) — Lyell's Principles of Geology (1830–33) gave constancy scientific legitimacy, conflating actualistic uniformitarianism (physical laws don't change) with substantive (Earth's current state is typical of all history). Thornhill: "As soon as they accepted global catastrophes in the dim past, the attitude should have changed to THE PRESENT IS NOT THE KEY TO THE PAST."

• Deep Time — Extending timescale to billions of years made the question of a recently-arrived Sun absurd.

The People's Sun vs. Priest's Sun

Sun as saviour originally carried a liberating message: Sun rose for everyone, demanding no intermediary. Constancy was the answer: an eternal Sun requires an eternal institution to administer it. Constantinople — City of Constancy — was headquarters of this programme.

Constansy Thesis

No source in Saturnian Cosmology, Fomenko, or Thunderbolts Project makes explicit connection between Latin constans and doctrine of solar constancy. Etymology is attested and ideological programme documented, but the claim that naming was deliberate is original to this investigation.
