12th Century: Birth of Christianity

In Fomenko's New Chronology, the first widely recorded and purely human event was the crucifixion of Andronicus Christ
in 1185. Biographies of Czar-Grad's 'Byzantine' Emperor Andronicus and the Great Russian
Prince Andrey Bogoliubsky exist as surviving testimonies about the
historical Christ which match up nicely to the redacted New Testament literature put out by the Catholic Church, but
differ in many significant details. For example, Christ was of
a race of hairy human giants similar to the
mythical Sasquatch.
He was born in the year 1152 AD (rather than around 4 BC), in modern-day Crimea (rather than Palestine), and his
mother Katya (later renamed Mary in the Latin bible) was originally from Rus' -- probably
from Rostov or nearby, and was buried in the Crimea. The far-eastern region
later called Cathaya was likely named after Katya, not the other way around --
a consequence of the Rus-Horde Empire centering its state religion on veneration of the Mother of God, long before it
ever centered on her Son.

During this period, the Deep State Holy Empire
spanned from Western Europe to Istanbul with Jerusalem #1 as their capital. Their slow-and-go plan was to spread a
centralized usury religion based on redacted old-testament narratives and an invisible creator lord which their priests
spoke for. This plan was foiled in less than a decade by Christ -- the Church's "prodigal child" -- who returned to Jerusalem proselytizing an anti-establishment religion promising freedom from tyranny and an end to
usury.

This forced the hand of the Deep State which saw no other option than to crucify Christ in a gruesome public display
within the tall gates of Troy.
The objective of the crucifixion was to put down his insurrection and make an example of its leader. But the plan
backfired catastrophically: Christ's brother died on the cross in his place, and Christ himself survived to found a
world empire that would challenge the Church's authority for centuries.

The Church regarded Christ exactly as the prodigal son parable describes: a wayward son who rejected his priestly inheritance,
abandoned the institution that raised him, and lived among commoners. In the Church's own retelling the prodigal son's "return" is recast as a voluntary sacrifice: he allows himself to be crucified, seeking forgiveness from the 'Father'.
In reality, Christ never sought forgiveness. He was captured and executed. Later when Christ was renamed Jesus and elevated to ‘Son of God’, the parable was reappropriated to make the execution look like a homecoming. This makes sense if Christ was 'coming home' to God. However, the final version recorded in Luke removed Christ as the identified son entirely. Now the prodigal son would become a fictional character in a story made up by Christ.

Two branches of Christianity. Revolution Despite Christ's Martyrdom states Fomenko's map, then steps past his 11th-century-forward horizon: the Istanbul church is treated here as far older than Christ, so the institution calling itself Apostolic cannot be reduced to his post-crucifixion "apostolic" faction alone. A tabular Fomenko-vs-extension breakdown (editor reference) lives at /history/chronology/investigations/two-branches-christianity-fomenko-vs-author.md.

Deep State, the Census, & Naming of Christ

The Giants and the Deep State

The normal-sized humans of the Deep State had a rocky history with the Giants — who remembered their enslavement by the
Pyramidal Empire. Giants were notoriously difficult to convert into Monotheism. They had their own religions, (large)
bibles, and a significant following of normal-sized humans. The Deep State knew they would never rule world religions
as long as the Giants existed. If they could only eliminate them all, then normal-sized humans would fall under the
influence of Deep State religions.

The Census

The Latin Bible contains a western perspective of the Crucifixion, a story originally written in the Cyrillic bible,
but many of the facts are the same. The story of Christ began with
the census
ordered by the religious leaders of Jerusalem in Istanbul. However, it is illogical to suggest that a pregnant woman
(who is due) would be forced to travel to take part in a census. That never happens in any census. Instead, the
census was of a very different nature; the Deep State was counting the giants of the region in preparation for a
planned genocide of giants.

At the same time, the Deep State prioritized the conversion of giants into Monotheism (if at all possible). They saw
the Baptism of the giant Andronicus Christ as a unique opportunity to spread their influence east. With the census, many
giants were fleeing the Istanbul region knowing what the census would mean for them if they stayed put — and many giants
were killed en route.

The Naming of Christ

Christ means "anointed one" — meaning he was anointed with oil as part of the ancient Deep State ritual
of Baptism. This was unique for only one reason — Christ and his mother
were large hairy humans from Rus', a land of giants that rarely
participated in any Deep State religious ceremonies. This land would birth the first world empire since the dark ages
and would come to be known as the Kingdom of Tartaria.

The Church was building a patriarchal religion — authority passed from God the Father, to Christ the Son, to the male
apostles, to the male priesthood. Christ's hairy giant mother, carrying the matrilineal tradition of Rus', threatened
this entire structure. In the eastern tradition she came from, religious heritage passed through the mother, not
the father. A pregnant "Wild Woman" fleeing the census represented everything the Deep State sought to eliminate.

See: Hairy Mary: The Wild Woman Who Threatened Patriarchal Religion

06.01.01-hairy-mary.md
========================================================================

Hairy Mary: The Wild Woman Who Threatened Patriarchal Religion

Wild Women were portrayed with full body hair, nursing children, living outside Church society. The iconography encodes memory of a real human phenotype.

Why Was Christ's Mother Depicted as Hairy?

Art historian Magdalena Łanuszka rejects the standard explanation that medieval artists confused Mary Magdalene with Mary of Egypt:

"I am pretty convinced that the medieval viewer, looking at a hirsute Mary Magdalene, most likely saw her as a Wild Woman!"

Wild Men and Wild Women (Wildweibchen) were a distinct iconographic tradition — hairy, self-sufficient humans in wilderness. The Met Museum holds Master ES's "Wild Woman with Unicorn." In medieval art, Wild Men "symbolised sinful humanity" — exactly what we'd expect if the Church sought to demonize a population it intended to eliminate. Łanuszka notes the hirsute Magdalene vanished from Western art when Wild Men lost popularity — the tradition ended because living memory of actual hairy humans was extinguished.

The Pregnant Hairy Woman Fleeing the Census

No census has required pregnant women to travel. Mary was not traveling to the census — she was fleeing it. The census targeted her kind: hairy giants from Rus'. In 15th-century Netherlandish paintings, Magdalene appears in unlaced maternity dresses — the period's pregnancy signal. In Rogier van der Weyden's Descent from the Cross, Magdalene wears a belt inscribed "JHESUS MARIA" — as if the unborn child bore its parents' names.

Christ's father is absent from every Gospel. The Church's "divine conception" may overlay simpler reality: the father was killed or captured in the census-genocide. Marriage as institution was created by the same Church hunting her people. Among self-sufficient, matrilineal Wild People, Church-sanctioned union meant nothing.

The Existential Threat: Matrilineal vs Patrilineal Religion

Christ's mother threatened the nascent Church because of lineage. In eastern Rus' tradition, religious heritage passed through the mother. The Church was building a patriarchal religion where authority flowed from God the Father to Christ the Son to male priests. A prominent female figure passing religious authority through the maternal line collapsed their claim.

The Church's solution: conflate all Marys into one domesticated Virgin; split threatening aspects into a separate sinful penitent; eliminate the Wild People — removing living evidence of the old matrilineal tradition.

The Trojan War: A War Over the "Woman"

Per Fomenko's reconstruction, the Bible and chronicles used "wives" to represent religions: "the cause of the Trojan = Tarquinian = Gothic war could have been a RELIGIOUS DISPUTE: whose 'wife' was better." The "woman" at the centre of the Trojan War was Mary — the religious tradition of Rus'. The conflict was between Eastern/matrilineal tradition and Western/patrilineal Deep State priesthood.

The hairy giants were dangerous because they were self-sufficient — able to live without buildings, money, or Church infrastructure. Christ's mother, fleeing the census with her unborn child, represented an older humanity that threatened the patriarchal church by existing. See The Census and Naming of Christ.

Sources

• Łanuszka, Magdalena. "Hairy Mary," Posztukiwania.

• Fomenko, A.T. "Chapter 3: The Trojan Horse." History: Fiction or Science?

06.02.00-1152-ce-historical-christ-is-born-in-crimea.md
========================================================================

Historical Christ is Born in Crimea

Christ's mother Katya was one of those attempting to flee the census. As a hairy giant from Rus', she knew the census was not a count but a prelude to genocide. She made it as far as Crimea before being caught. The locals took pity as she laboured through a difficult delivery. The language barrier prevented them from establishing who the father was — many assumed she was a prostitute, or a virgin. Her husband may have been killed by smaller humans, and she refused to speak of it. Unable to fit inside buildings, she was placed in a stable where locals performed a cesarean section.

The Cesarean Section and "Perpetual Virginity"

Fomenko demonstrates that "caesarean" derives from Christ's birth. In Church Slavonic liturgy, the irmos reads: "Him, who before the Sun shined, and in flesh came from virgin's side, infinitely incarnated, we praise You, Mother of God." The words describe a cesarean section. Greek PARTHENOS (virgin) originates from Slavonic POROT', meaning "to cut open." The claim Mary "remained a virgin after giving birth" is literally true under cesarean: the birth canal was never used.

According to eastern chronicles, the procedure stabbed infant Christ while still in the womb, generating traditions of the Holy Lance and Holy Grail. This birth wound may have been conflated with the lance of the 'Roman' soldier at the Crucifixion.

Eventually authorities agreed to let the giants live, provided they accepted Baptism into Monotheism.

The Magi: Rus-Horde Rulers Accept Christ

In Christ's epoch, Rus' adopted Christianity wholly and instantly. Fomenko identifies the Magi as three Rus-Horde rulers: Russian Czar Vladimir (Balthazar), Czaritsa Malka — his mother (Melchior), and Cossack Ataman (Caspar). A Cologne Cathedral was erected in their honour, housing the Shrine of the Three Kings. Medieval images depict the adoration against banners displaying a crescent and star — symbol of Czar-Grad (Jerusalem). In the Cologne Shrine, two Magi are bearded men, but the third is clearly female — Queen Malka, later masculinised by Western artists uncomfortable with Slavic origins.

Christ's Education and Conflict with the Church

Christ traveled with his family to Jerusalem in Istanbul, where he trained as a Rabbi. Rabbis endlessly deliberated to reconcile ancient Monotheism with the polytheistic religions they were absorbing. This backfired: Christ and his mother remained unconvinced of Church authority, deferring to her eastern Rus' heritage. Due to Christ's clerical criticisms, his family was driven from Jerusalem. Matthew's Gospel preserves a redacted version in the Holy Family's flight from King Herod to Egypt — in eastern records, they were fleeing Istanbul persecution back to Rus'.

After returning home, Christ developed a following around hybridised teachings — parables demonstrating people were born free and needed no Church or God to mediate their lives. This spread quickly to Istanbul, challenging Monotheism's foundations. The Deep State laid plans to capture him. He was invited to a Passover celebration in Jerusalem, arrested, tried, and crucified. The charge was insurrection — not for challenging Monotheism, but for allegedly being King of the Jews, a title he never claimed.

See: Historical Christ is Crucified in Istanbul

06.03.00-christ-is-crucified-in-istanbul.md
========================================================================

Christ is Crucified in Istanbul

Jupiter and Saturn aligned as the Star of Bethlehem on December 21, 2020, marking the 840-Year Anniversary of the Crucifixion in Constantinople of Andronicus-Christ of the Crimea.

With planetary orbits stable for over a hundred years, it was now possible for the first time to predict future eclipses. The date and time of the crucifixion was purposefully chosen on a solar eclipse to magnify the psychological impact of the event. To the medieval mind it meant that God passed over the judgment and did not intervene to save Christ. This lie sufficed and his followers scattered in fear. The crucifixion of Christ was designed to end the influence of Giants like Christ by allowing smaller humans to poke, stab, and laugh at the giants with impunity while they were bound to the crosses.

The Site: Yoros = Jerusalem = Golgotha

The crucifixion took place on Beykoz mountain, on the Asiatic shore of the Bosphorus, near the fortress of Yoros — an abbreviation of Ierosalim (Jerusalem). This fortress controlled the narrowest part of the Bosphorus close to the entrance to the Black Sea. To this day, an enormous fenced plot (approximately 3 by 17 metres) is known as the "grave of Yusha (Jesus)" — not a real grave, but a memorial marking the site of the crucifixion. For a full account of Yoros as First Jerusalem, see Deep State Centralize World Religion at Jerusalem.

It is notable that in many paintings, icons and frescoes, Christ's crucifixion is shown with a background of a big sea strait or wide river — not an open sea. Artists with accurate recollection of the original story would have depicted the Bosphorus strait as a significant part of the landscape, as Beykoz mountain sits right on the shore with a clear view of the European shore where central Constantinople is situated.

The Turin Shroud: Physical Evidence

The most significant physical evidence for the 1185 dating is the Turin Shroud. In 1998, radiocarbon dating was performed by three independent laboratories — Oxford University, Arizona State University, and the Swiss Federal Institute of Technology (Zurich). The result: approximately the XI-XII centuries. Fomenko argues the most probable date is the second half of the XII century, corresponding precisely with the 1185 crucifixion. Christ's body was wrapped in the Shroud, which then found its way to Russia, where it was folded so that only the Holy Face was visible — becoming the icon known as the Image of the Savior Not-Made-By-Hands, used as a military banner by Yaroslavl, Tver' and Moscow princes. The Shroud reached Turin only in the XVII century, after the fall of the Great Empire, when formerly seized relics could be displayed without fear that the Horde would reclaim them.

The historical Christ — a giant of the Rus-Horde — is crucified on Joshua's Hill (Beykoz/Golgotha) in Constantinople/Istanbul by the Deep State operatives.

See: Alleged Resurrection

06.03.01-the-brother-on-the-cross.md
========================================================================

The Brother on the Cross

What the Church Does Not Want You to Know

After 6 hours on the cross, the condemned man passed out and was stabbed by a soldier's spear. The body was removed by Christ's own family and brought into a nearby cave. The smaller humans, fearing retribution, did not follow. Within 3 days, the family departed for Crimea without fanfare. There is no reason to assume the cave was used for burial.

Then Christ was seen alive — many times, in many places, in Asia and America.

The Japanese Tradition: Isukiri Died in Christ's Place

In an alternate version preserved in Shingo, Japan, Christ did not die on the cross. His younger brother Isukiri took his place, while Jesus escaped east across Siberia to northern Japan.

Shingo: purported resting place of Jesus and residence of his descendants, the Sawaguchi family.

When Jesus Christ was 21 years old, he came to Japan and pursued knowledge of divinity for 12 years. He went back to Judea at age 33 and engaged in his mission. However people in Judea would not accept Christ's preaching. Instead, they arrested him and tried to crucify him. His younger brother Isukiri casually took Christ's place and ended his life on the cross.

This timeline adopts the Japanese tradition: Isukiri died on the cross. Christ survived. The crowd could not tell two giant brothers apart — giants were alien to smaller humans. See: Historical Christ dies at age 106 in Japan

Romulus and Remus: The Same Story, Redacted

Fomenko identifies Romulus as a partial reflection of Christ and Remus as John the Baptist (reflection #13 in Fomenko's 39 phantoms). Two brothers raised together, one dies, the survivor founds a world empire:

• Romulus (Christ) survives and founds Rome (the Rus-Horde Empire)

• Remus (John the Baptist / Isukiri) dies, founding the mythic basis of the new state

The "ancient Rome" founded by Romulus and Remus is the rise of Russia-Horde in XIII–XIV centuries. Fomenko documents extensively that ancient authors constantly confused Christ and John the Baptist — precisely what we'd expect if the record was redacted to suppress the most embarrassing moment in Church history: they crucified the "King of the Jews" only to have him walk out alive and found the largest medieval empire.

The Church buried the brother's existence, splitting him into dozens of phantom characters (John the Baptist, Remus, and many others), rewriting the survival as a supernatural resurrection requiring faith rather than an earthly escape requiring only two brothers who looked alike.

The Backfire

The Monotheists who ordered the crucifixion didn't realize the revolution would survive. Before Christ, martyring popular figures had always defeated movements. Christ's movement may have succeeded because the leader was never actually killed.

By crucifying "Christ," the Church declared he was not the messiah. The medieval mind concluded the opposite — that Christ was the messiah denied by the Jews. The crucifixion created their worst nightmare: a human savior of eastern bloodlines worshipped as a god by the west.

Fomenko documents the duplications: 113 reflections of Christ, 59 of Virgin Mary, 39 of John the Baptist, and 61 of Judas Iscariot.

06.04.00-revolution-despite-martyrdom.md
========================================================================

Revolution Despite Christ's Martyrdom

The survival of the Christian revolution was not caused by a supernatural resurrection — it was that Christ walked away from the execution alive. He returned from Istanbul to Crimea with his brother Isukiri's body; the congregation of ordinary-sized people who followed him carried the movement.

Fomenko: the two branches

Fomenko's chronologia describes two Christianities after the crucifixion. Royal (Ancestral) Christianity is the religion of Christ's relatives and the imperial clan — they honor Christ as God but treat their own blood as sacred too, like a family of quarrelling gods (Egyptian and Greek god-king parallels). Apostolic Christianity is disciples and commoners: no royal descent, refusal to worship emperors as gods, and in his telling many early martyrs. The later church's claim to be "Apostolic" only makes sense if another Christianity once existed beside it.

He traces a long split: royal strains toward polytheistic / Gnostic forms; apostolic strains toward Judaism, Catholicism, and Islam as we read them now. Circumcision and Paul's war on the Judaizers sit in that fight — one side forcing old covenant marks on converts, Paul calling that slavery again (Galatians). His reconstruction of this drama is anchored in the High Middle Ages forward; it does not reconstruct how long the Istanbul temple church had already operated before Christ appeared.

Where the vocabulary and the institution diverge

Elsewhere this chronology treats the church in Istanbul as ancient — long predating the 12th-century Christ. If the institution survived centuries before Christ, its rites are not invented by the disciples; they continue older forms. The same chronology reads their function across the Dark Ages as control of food and money, bondage of people, and mediation of an invisible creator — the pattern the Deep State holy empire elsewhere describes. Christ threatened that ledger, not only a doctrinal dispute.

Royal Christianity names the movement around the historical figure: popular, not palace bloodline. People honored him as king because he spoke for them; Davidic genealogy fits better as a later scriptural retrofit (and met longer resistance in Rus). The institution that branded itself Apostolic was already adversary at his arrival, not a persecuted flock of his invention. After the crucifixion, roughly two centuries pass in which the establishment can act as if he had not mattered; then he is folded in — next savior and Son of God under the same invisible-deity frame, with celestial emphasis shifting (Sun, Mars) as astrology required. The name Apostolic works as cover for continuity with the pre-Christ cult.

Who suffered — and who were not martyrs

The textbook picture — apostles crushed by "royal" power — fails if Royal is taken here as the people's Christianity (king in spirit, not dynasty). The bloodiest clash is Crusader revenge for the crucifixion against the circumcision party, Istanbul temple apparatus, and Judaizers — not "Christ's royal kin vs humble apostolic masses." See First Crusade and the Trojan War and The Brother on the Cross.

Church and Fomenko often cast the early apostolic side as martyrs. For the First Crusade layer, that label does not hold: those on the losing side understood guilt around the crucifixion and faced something close to peasant or mob justice — repentance was common; some were killed anyway. That is punishment, not martyrdom. Martyrdom would require dying for the justice of the cross — insisting Christ deserved execution. No record of such a stance among those swept up in revenge has been surfaced. Without it, martyr is the wrong word.

It is for freedom that Christ has set us free… if you let yourselves be circumcised, Christ will be of no value to you at all. — Galatians 5:1–2 (NIV)

Rus-Horde and the Deep State trap

Foundations for Rus-Horde defiance of Istanbul; redacted into Medina, "ancient Rome," etc. Yoros = Jerusalem = Troy. Deep State, Census, Naming. Trap at the walls: First Crusade, Russian Horde Empire.

Real Christianity reads as popular revolution against ritual deep state. The institutional Apostolic line reads as old church that ignored then absorbed Christ. Narratives of martyrdom in the revenge wars obscure guilt and rough justice — not death in defense of the crucifixion's righteousness.

06.05.00-the-first-crusade-trojan-war.md

The First Crusade & Trojan War

Revenge for the Crucifixion (~1196 CE)

The humiliation of the Crucifixion triggered a violent reaction across the gathering Rus-Horde. Within eleven years the Russians launched the First Crusade to recapture the Holy Sepulcher and avenge Christ.

Fomenko notes that medieval Church sources assert "immediately after Jesus' Resurrection, Caesar's army went on a Crusade to Jerusalem and seized it" — dismissed by Scaligerian historians as "vapid conjecture" because no Roman Crusade occurred in the first century. In the New Chronology, the sources are correct: they describe the late 12th–early 13th century campaigns. The so-called First Crusade, today dated to 1096, is correctly dated to ~1196. Fomenko stresses: "the Crusaders were convinced they were avenging not the descendants of the Savior's executioners, but these executioners themselves."

Those on the Istanbul / temple / Judaizer side in this violence are not martyrs in the sense developed in Revolution Despite Christ's Martyrdom: guilt was acknowledged, repentance common, mob- or peasant-level justice; some died anyway. Martyrdom would require dying for the claim that the crucifixion was just — no such record is appealed to.

Not all giants endorsed the Revenge Crusade. Older, wiser giants knew it was a trap and that two wrongs make no right. The young giants (up to 40 years old) marched before the full army was ready — this first wave is remembered as the Children's Crusade — and were killed or enslaved. The older giants then assumed command and drew more cautious plans.

Infantry and guerrilla are later lexical fossils of the same world: infantry traces to Italian infante (foot soldier, youth); guerrilla is Spanish for “little war,” applied centuries later to irregular resistance — a pattern giant-line survivors fit once formed battle failed. Full comparison (mainstream vs this read): Giants, Infantry, Guerrilla — Etymology appendix; dossier: giants-infantry-guerrilla-etymology-investigation.md.

The Trojan War

As the Revenge Crusade deepened, it turned religious: a new faith was forming around Christ and his mother Mary, while the Trojan side worshipped the ancient Deep State goddess Aphrodite. Hence the tradition that the Trojan War was triggered "over a woman." A decade of fighting killed many giants. The Iliad traces some giant lineages back to planet Mars — the origin of the Jotunn.

The First Great War

This was the first great war of the revenge crucifixion era: Rus-Horde giants and allies avenging Christ versus the Istanbul–Troy deep-state side — temple apparatus, circumcision party, and smaller-human factions loyal to the established cult (Aphrodite / Deep State goddess), not Fomenko's schematic of royal kin vs post-crucifixion apostolic commoners at this layer. See Revolution Despite Christ's Martyrdom.

The war concluded at the Battle of Troy/Jerusalem/TsarGrad with the giant Greeks winning. Unable to breach TsarGrad's walls, the giants constructed a giant-scaled wooden horse as a tribute-offering and feigned retreat. The Trojans brought the horse inside, unable to imagine that the giants had cultivated smaller-human allies willing to die on their behalf — loyalty born directly from the Crucifixion of Christ.

07.01.02-the-giants-of-the-rus-horde.md

The Giants of the Rus-Horde

The word Horde (Russian: Orda) derives from RAT' (рать), meaning army; RUTHENIA meant RATNAYA — a military country. "Mongol" comes from MNOGO (много, "many") or Greek MEGALION ("great") (Fomenko, ch.3 §7). The mistranslation of Orda as "horde" — conjuring disorderly savages — obscures a disciplined imperial civilization whose leadership caste were giant, hairy humans.

The Giant Leadership

The Rus-Horde's ruling class — descendants of the Wild People — were of enormous stature, covered in body hair, and venerated as living connections to the pre-catastrophe world. Christ himself, born of the hairy giant Mary of Rus', founded this dynasty. They possessed knowledge systems, religious traditions, and physical capabilities that made them ungovernable by the Deep State.

The Codex Gigas: A Giant's Bible

The Codex Gigas — "Giant Book" — is the largest extant medieval manuscript: 92 cm tall, 50 cm wide, 74.8 kg, 310 vellum leaves from ~160 donkey skins. A 36-inch, 165-pound book cannot be used at a normal desk — it was made for a giant reader. The "Devil" depicted inside — green-skinned, hairy, horned — is a demonised portrait of the race the book served. The first page includes Early Cyrillic and Glagolitic alphabets — scripts of Rus'.

Three Wars Against the Giants

• The Crusade (early 12th century) — first campaign against giant populations, triggered by Christ's Crucifixion and a Deep State census targeting giants near Istanbul. Infantry, guerrilla, and the Children's Crusade label: Giants, Infantry, Guerrilla — Etymology; narrative hook for the Children's wave — First Crusade & Trojan War.

• Battle of Kulikovo (1380) — cannon technology defeated the giants. "Royal" Christianity fell to Apostolic Christianity.

• Pugachev Rebellion (1773–1775) — the final war. Pugachev led giants and humans against the Romanovs. Defeat by directed energy weapons ended giant civilization. Remaining giants were instantly fossilised and their bones reclassified as dinosaur fossils.

The Smithsonian Cover-Up

The Smithsonian Institution, founded 1846 — one generation after the MudFlood — excavated giant skeletal remains across North America. 19th-century newspapers describe 7–12 foot skeletons across the Ohio Valley, Mississippi region, and Southwest. None are on public display.

Universal Giant Traditions

Velikovsky: "The traditions of peoples all over the world are quite unanimous in asserting that at an earlier time a race of giants lived on the earth, that most of the race were destroyed in great catastrophes." The Japanese Ainu, Aztec Quinametzin, Basque Jentilak, Hebrew Nephilim, Og of Bashan (iron bedstead ~13.5 × 6 feet) — the pattern repeats everywhere.

Modern Predictive Programming

Erasure is maintained by predictive programming: Jack the Giant Killer, The BFG, Attack on Titan, Harry Potter — giants are dangerous and better eliminated. Jack and the Beanstalk was first published 1807, 32 years after Pugachev's defeat — by the generation witnessing actual extermination.

The Lineage from Christ to Pugachev

Christ (Andronicus I) was a giant from Rus'. His descendants founded the Horde Empire. Three wars progressively reduced the giant population. After 1775, buildings built for the war against giants — including castles lost their primary function and became romanticized stately homes, cultural heritage sites, and tourist attractions. The Codex Gigas sits in the Swedish Royal Library: 36 inches tall, 165 pounds. It was not made for us.

Sources: Fomenko, How It Was in Reality; Codex Gigas; Velikovsky, Giants

07.02.00-1258-ce-historical-christ-dies.md

Historical Christ dies

According to Japanese legend,
Christ survived his crucifixion, travelled east to Japan, and
died
at Kirisuto no haka at the age of 106. See Alleged Resurrection
for the full account including the sign text at the tomb and the alternate history of Christ's brother Isukiri.

The Legend of Shingo

The village of Shingo in Aomori Prefecture, Japan, claims to be
the final resting place of Jesus Christ. According to the local tradition — preserved in the Takenouchi Documents — Christ
first arrived in Japan at the age of 21 to study theology under a great master near Mount Fuji. He returned to Judea
at 33, was arrested, but escaped crucifixion by substituting his brother Isukiri on the cross. Christ then fled east
across Siberia and Alaska, eventually reaching the northern coast of Japan. He settled in what is now Shingo, married a
Japanese woman named Miyuko, fathered three daughters, and lived as a rice farmer under the name Daitenku Taro Jurai.

A sign at the twin burial mounds (Kirisuto no haka) reads:

"When Jesus Christ was 21 years old, he came to Japan and pursued knowledge of divinity for 12 years. He went back
to Judea at age 33, and after his crucifixion, his brother Isukiri took his place and Jesus who escaped from the
execution traversed the land of Siberia to Alaska, and came to the Aomori Place, where he died at the age of 106."

The village holds an annual Christ Festival (Kirisuto Matsuri) every June, where participants dance around the burial
mounds in traditional costume. Whether this is genuine transmission or later fabrication, the tradition is notable for
preserving the same core claim as the Fomenko chronology: that Christ survived the crucifixion and lived to old age.

In the New Chronology, Christ's birth in 1152 CE + 106 years = death in 1258 CE, placing his passing within the
period of the Rus-Horde Empire's consolidation — the very empire his teachings had set in motion.

According to Japanese tradition, Christ survived the crucifixion, travelled east to Japan, and died peacefully at Kirisuto no haka at the age of 106.

First Olympic Games

In memory of the religious plurality that followed the Crusades the Olympic Games would be held every 4 years throughout
the Empire. Coliseums were built to host the events in western states like France, Italy, Tunisia, and Croatia. They
were never used for gladiator fights which mostly occurred
on public squares outside the boundaries of imperial law.

De Grazia: The Games as Post-Catastrophe Reassembly

De Grazia notes in Chaos and Creation that the first Olympic Games "marked a reassembly of Greeks" after a period of
devastating celestial conflict — the age when Mars repeatedly approached the Earth. The founder of the games was
"none other than Hercules, alter ego of planet Mars." In Scaligerian chronology the first Games date to 776 BCE; in
the Fomenko chronology this reassembly corresponds to the post-Crusade pacification of the late 13th century CE — the
same impulse to mark a new era of civilization after devastating conflict.

The Games were accompanied by the adoption of a new calendar. De Grazia writes: "Nabonassar, an obscure king of Babylon,
gave his name to a new era of the calendar in the year 747 B.C." and notes that two Dutch scientists reviewed
radiocarbon, tree ring, and varve studies pointing to "a considerable lengthening of the solar year, from perhaps 280
to 365 days, around 780 B.C." Whether dated to the 8th century BCE or the 13th century CE, the pattern is consistent:
the Olympic Games inaugurated a new calendar marking the end of a catastrophic era and the beginning of ordered
civilization.

The coliseums built across the empire — massive arenas in France, Italy, Tunisia, and Croatia — are conventionally dated
to "ancient Rome" but the Fomenko chronology attributes them to the Rus-Horde construction program of the 13th–14th
centuries. Their architectural uniformity across vast distances speaks to a single centralized empire, not independent
city-states separated by centuries.

Coliseums were built across the Empire to host the Games — in France, Italy, Tunisia, and Croatia — celebrating religious plurality across the Rus-Horde.

14th Century: The Great Expansion — Triumph and Betrayal

The 14th century is the story of two empires inhabiting the same body. Externally, the Rus-Horde reached its zenith. Internally, the court was already infiltrated by forces the Crusade sought to destroy. By century's end, cracks erupted in civil war at the Battle of Kulikovo.

The External Triumph

By the late 13th century, Georgiy Danilovich (Grand Prince of Moscow) — recorded in western/eastern sources as Genghis Khan — united Vladimir and Suzdal Rus' under Lord Novgorod the Great. His brother Ivan Danilovich Kalita (recorded as Batu Khan and Pope Innocent III) continued expansion and, per Fomenko, founded the Vatican and Rome — VATI-KAN being Latinised Batu Khan.

By mid-century Russian cavalry invaded Europe, Africa, and India. In Western sources the Rus-Horde appears as the Habsburg Empire of the XIV–XVI cc. — Habsburg possibly from Slavonic HAB (NEW) + BURG (CITY) = Novgorod. The Horde chose Florence as Italian capital; Imperial headquarters were established separately, forming Rome.

With expansion came monumental construction (14th–16th c.): Tartarian cathedrals across Europe and North America; the Great Wall of China and Hagia Sophia (= Temple of Solomon / Czar-Grad); the Great Sphinx and Pyramids at Giza (geopolymer concrete by Cossack-Hordian Mamelukes); megalithic Baalbek and the great pyramids of Central America.

In Egypt, a cemetery was established near Giza to inter mummified Czar-Khans. Dry sand preserved bodies transported across the Mediterranean — the "River Styx." The 30 Egyptian pharaoh dynasties are phantom reflections of 13th–16th century Russian czar-khans.

The Hundred Years War Begins

The conventional Hundred Years' War (1337–1453) is, in New Chronology, Hordian pacification campaigns in Western Europe. Many vassal regions chafed under military tagma (blood-tribute) and taxation.

Fomenko notes multiplication of place-names during conquests caused conflicts to be recorded twice. Transfer of Russian-Turkic names (Cossack → Gascon; Tatar → Tartar provinces of France; Rus → Prussia, Persia, Paris) obscured origins. Coordinated resistance suggests an organised network fanning the flames — inseparable from the religious schism.

The campaign dragged on a hundred years; conclusion belongs to the 15th century, when the Fall of Czar-Grad (1453) marked both war's end and the Deep State's pivot from resistance to infiltration.

The Worm in the Court

The giants who led the Horde ruled by legitimacy and physical authority but were poorly suited to court politics. Smaller humans staffing the court — scribes, translators, physicians, priests — excelled at intrigue. Former Deep State priests who survived the Crusade wormed into the court as advisors. Fomenko notes Georgiy Danilovich (Genghis Khan) was "murdered by conspirators" early in his reign.

The full bloodline compromise — through strategic marriage, the Heresy of the Judaizers, and Esther = Elena Voloshanka — came in the XVI century. But groundwork was laid here.

08.01.00-1300-ce-european-religious.md

European Religious Schism

The Great Empire initially practiced a state-tolerated folk spirituality integrating planetary veneration, ancestor worship, and pragmatic governance. As the Empire expanded, a systematic split emerged: Royal Christianity and Apostolic Christianity — two competing ideological systems that eventually produced rival civilizations.

Royal Christianity

The rulers' ideology, directly serving Imperial legitimacy. Core tenets:

• The Emperor is the divinely appointed Christ/Czar — God's authority on Earth through Imperial lineage

• Religious and civil law are inseparable — The Empire embodies both spiritual and legal order

• Centralized hierarchy — local governors are extensions of Imperial will

Royal Christianity prioritized order, tribute, and expansion. It was the internal ideology of the ruling class, not a missionary project.

Apostolic Christianity

A parallel movement emerged privileging teaching over governance and apostolic succession over imperial appointment. Core tenets:

• Authority derives from Peter and the Apostles — not from emperors

• The spiritual hierarchy is independent of civil power — bishops answer to Rome/Constantinople, not local governors

• Universal mission — Apostles' Christianity is for all peoples, not just Imperial subjects

The Structural Schism

The division's significance: it created two branches of government.

Royal Christians saw the schism as rebellion — provincial governors claiming autonomy under religious pretext.

Apostolic Christians saw it as fidelity to the monotheistic creator god over the Tsar — the Empire's corruption required an independent church.

This tension intensified from 1300 CE onward as Europe fractured under internal conflict and the Apostolic institutions accumulated their own power centers.

The Deep State Exploits the Schism

On the Deep State's formation and CE role, see Humanity cast out and Deep State, Census, Naming. Fomenko identifies "Judaic capital" (what we call the Deep State) as exploiting this division. Pre-existing financial networks operating across Imperial borders found opportunity in religious fragmentation. Strategy:

• Finance both sides — Protestant, Catholic, and Orthodox factions all needed capital

• Weaponize religious difference — Wars justify borrowing and create debt cycles

• Survive regime changes — Networks persist while dynasties rise and fall

The Deep State's power emerged not from conquering territory but from becoming indispensable to everyone who did.

Seeds of 1492

By the 14th century the structural preconditions for the Empire's fragmentation were established:

• Royal vs. Apostolic conflict provided permanent fault line

• Financial networks positioned themselves as power brokers

• Regional governors recognized the potential of independence under Apostolic legitimacy

The Great Expansion (1300s–1400s) paradoxically accelerated the fracture: the farther the Empire spread, the harder Rus-Horde found it to enforce loyalty. The provinces that would become Western Europe began forming distinct identities — ideologically, financially, and eventually militarily positioned for the 16th-century breakaway.

Giants are defeated at the Battle of Kulikovo

The Battle of Kulikovo (~1380 CE) was the decisive turning-point of recorded history. The giants of the Horde were defeated by the new "Marathon" cannon technology of smaller humans. Royal (Hereditary) Christianity fell; Apostles' Christianity triumphed. The giants lost authority over the Rus-Horde Empire and became permanent enemies of the state: enslaved, hunted, eradicated.

The Battle of Kulikovo marks the moment cannon technology overcame armored giants — remembered as David vs Goliath and the Battle of Marathon.

The Weapon That Changed Everything

Metal cannons — deployed en masse for the first time — could penetrate heavy armour that had made giants invincible. The theological significance was immediate: Dmitry Donskoi = Constantine the Great baptised the Empire into Apostles' Christianity and established hereditary czarist succession — replacing the older system where giant clan-leaders rotated power.

Thirty Phantom Reflections

Fomenko identifies Kulikovo as the most duplicated event in history, reflected across at least 30 phantom copies:

• David and Goliath (1 Samuel 17) — A young king defeats a giant with a "sling" (= cannon).

• The Gallic War giant — Roman histories record a monstrous champion slain by a smaller soldier; the "barbarian" army collapses immediately.

• King Arthur and the giant — Geoffrey of Monmouth has Arthur fight "a fierce giant" at Mont-Saint-Michel, then conquer the continent.

• Zeus and the Titans — The gods overthrow the Titans with help from the Cyclops — a metaphor for cannons: a single "eye" (= muzzle) hurling thunderbolts (= cannonballs).

• Battle of Marathon (490 BCE) — "Javelins" defeat a vastly larger army. Greek city-states (smaller humans) prevail against the Persian Empire (the Horde). Chronological distance (~1870 years) is a classic Fomenko shift.

• Constantine defeats Maxentius — Fomenko's primary identification: Donskoi is Constantine I; the Roman Empire's conversion to Christianity is the Horde's conversion after Kulikovo.

Each narrative preserves a common skeleton: a smaller champion overcomes giants with a new weapon or divine intervention. The sheer number of duplicates testifies to the event's traumatic importance.

The Giant Rises Against Church and State

An extraordinary document detailing British government corruption — sinecures and privileges of army, church, and ruling class. By John Wade (1832).

Wade's Extraordinary Black Book was published 1832 — over 400 years after Kulikovo — yet its frontispiece depicts a giant falling victim to the "marathon" techniques of more numerous smaller humans. If memory of the Horde's giant-led military class survived in radical circles, the imagery is not metaphor — it is a record.

The defeat set the stage for two centuries of struggle. Deep State agents, already in the Kremlin court, worked to corrupt Apostles' Christianity from within — culminating in the Heresy of the Judaizers and the eventual splitting of the Empire in the 16th century.

Where giant lines could no longer hold set-piece battles, irregular war — what modern languages later called guerrilla (little war) — became the remaining option. See Giants, Infantry, Guerrilla — Etymology.

08.02.01-european-castles-fortresses-against-giants.md
========================================================================

European Castles — Fortresses Against Giants

The Standard Narrative Falls Apart

Mainstream history frames European castles as feudal military strongholds for human warfare. One problem: most castles in England, France, Ireland, and Scotland were never involved in conflicts or sieges. Wikipedia, citing Coulson (2003), admits the "domestic life" of castles is "a neglected facet."

When gunpowder artillery arrived (15th century), castles became indefensible overnight. Constantinople's walls fell to Ottoman cannon in six weeks (1453). Why were these immense structures so poorly optimised against artillery? Because they were designed against giants.

The Castle-Building Window and the Giant Timeline

Peak castle construction spans the 11th through 14th centuries — precisely when the Rus-Horde Empire and its giant leadership caste were active. The Battle of Kulikovo (1380) marked the decisive defeat of giants by cannon — and castle construction entered sharp decline immediately after.

Features That Make Sense Against Giants

Elevation and Inaccessibility: Hilltops, cliff edges, rocky outcrops, tidal islands — maximising difficulty for large, heavy creatures.

Wall Thickness Over Height: Walls typically 3 metres thick, 12 metres tall. Against humans with ladders, thickness is wasted. Against a giant who can strike or push a wall, mass is critical.

Narrow Entrances and Passages: Lengthened passages, multiple portcullises, sharp turns — a creature of enormous size physically cannot fit through turning passages.

Moats: Human armies routinely bridged or drained them. Against a giant, a deep ditch is devastating: a creature weighing several times more than a human sinks in soft ground and becomes exposed.

Keeps and Tower Houses: Keep entrances on the second storey (accessible only by removable ladders), spiral staircases that physically exclude any oversized creature.

Castle Stalker — Case Study

Castle Stalker is a tower house on a tidal islet in Loch Laich, Scotland — built c. 1320. Maximally defensible against large land creatures, minimally defensible against humans with boats. In 700 years it has never been sieged — it changed hands c. 1620 through a drunken bet. The name derives from Gaelic Stalcaire ("hunter"), suggesting a hunting lodge where the "wildlife" required stone walls and a tidal moat.

The Pattern Across Europe

Hundreds of tower houses across Scotland, Ireland, Wales, and western France share this profile: remote inaccessible locations, disproportionately thick walls, virtually no military history. Wikipedia estimates 75,000–100,000 castles across western Europe — defying purely military explanation for inter-human warfare, but sensible for a continent shared with giants.

The Gunpowder Transition

Once cannon proliferated, castles were abandoned wholesale — replaced by low-profile star forts with completely different philosophy. This discontinuity is not evolution but replacement: two engineering responses to two different threats. Once cannon defeated giants at Kulikovo (1380), castles had served their purpose.

Outstanding Questions

Further investigation: giant remains in castle archaeological contexts; geographic correlation between castle density and giant folklore; Asian/Middle Eastern equivalents.

08.03.00-imperial-technology-knowledge.md
========================================================================

Imperial Technology & Knowledge

The Great 14th-century Expansion was technological conquest as much as military. The Horde's ability to build monuments still standing, forge metals modern metallurgists struggle to replicate, and project force across three continents rested on unified knowledge Scaligerian history fragmented into "Greek," "Arabic," "Egyptian," "Indian," and "Chinese" strands. Fomenko restores the picture: one Empire, one knowledge tradition, deliberately shattered by later chroniclers. Guild transmission and post-fracture lodge form: The Masons; Freemasonry: From Operative to Speculative.

Geopolymer Concrete and the Philosopher's Stone

Monumental construction of this era — Egyptian pyramids, Hagia Sophia, Baalbek, Great Wall — shares a secret: geopolymer concrete. Fomenko identifies this as the Philosopher's Stone (chronologia.org Ch. 4 §18). Not mystical — a chemical binder that, added to crushed stone debris and water, produced artificial stone indistinguishable from natural rock. The Horde could cast enormous blocks in situ (eliminating impossible quarrying of 1,000+ ton Baalbek monoliths), reproduce any shape, work at enormous scale and speed.

The "alchemical" tradition — obsessive coded search for transformative substance — is garbled memory of this lost technology. When Empire fractured in 17th century and formula was lost, later scholars could only interpret surviving texts as mysticism.

Cannon Technology — The Cross and the Cannon

Fomenko connects cannon directly to the Cross (chronologia.org Ch. 4 §27): Russian word for cannon (pushka) and cross-shaped gun carriage are linked. Imperial banners carried the cross as symbol of their decisive weapon. Combined with Damask steel, cannon artillery made Horde unstoppable by early 15th century.

The "Arabic Golden Age" — A Single Knowledge Base

No separate "Arab" civilization — Arabic-speaking scholars were part of same Empire. Arabic inscriptions appear on Russian imperial weapons of 14th–16th centuries. Horde coins bear both Slavonic and Arabic. "Damascus steel" derives from Mosokh = Moscow, not Syrian city. "Greek → Arabic → Latin" translation chain was not civilizations but administrative languages within one Empire.

The "Islamic Golden Age" dissolves into what it was: scientific wing of the Horde, staffed by scholars writing in Arabic (one of Empire's co-languages alongside Slavonic, Turkic, Greek). When Empire split, each successor state claimed "its own" ancient tradition — Arabs took Arabic texts, Greeks the Greek ones, Latins the translations.

Fomenko demonstrates Roman numerals originated as Russian carpenter and builder marks (chronologia.org Ch. 4 §20) — practical tally notation for marking timber and stone. These marks spread across Europe during Great Expansion and were later attributed to fictional "Ancient Rome."

Alchemy and Metallurgy

Medieval alchemy was degraded residue of Horde industrial metallurgy: tin, copper, bronze on industrial scale; iron smelting for cannon and armour; "transmutation of metals" encoding real processes like cupellation (extracting gold from lead ore). Astronomical knowledge was collected by Horde-era observers — essential for navigation and fleet movements. Backdating by ~1,200 years was part of programme creating fictional "Ancient World."

08.03.01-geopolymer-concrete-horde-secret.md
========================================================================

Geopolymer Concrete: The Horde's Construction Secret

Canonical treatment: This article covers the geopolymer concrete evidence. For the broader question of what pyramids were and why they were built, see Pyramids: Myths vs Reality. For Fomenko's limitations on this topic, see Fomenko and the Pyramid Question.

The Davidovich Discovery

French chemist Joseph Davidovich founded the Geopolymer Institute in 1979 and proved through years of laboratory analysis that the blocks of the Great Pyramid — along with countless Egyptian statues, sarcophagi, and vessels — are artificial stone (Davidovich, refs [1086]–[1093] in Fomenko). The method: grind soft limestone into powder, mix with Nile clay (aluminium oxide), natron (sodium carbonate), and water. The slurry is poured into wooden moulds and hardens at room temperature into stone indistinguishable from natural rock.

This is not modern grey concrete. Geopolymeric concrete can replicate any geological material — limestone, granite, diorite, basalt. As Davidovich wrote: "Geologists unfamiliar with the capabilities of geopolymerisation mistake geopolymeric concrete for natural stone."

Herodotus Describes Moulds, Not Block-Dragging

Fomenko points out that Herodotus's famous description of pyramid construction — when read carefully — describes mobile wooden casings used to lift and cast concrete blocks level by level (chronologia.org Ch. 19 §6). Egyptologists interpreted "wooden machines" as ramps and sledges. But the text describes casings moved from tier to tier — casting moulds.

The Physical Evidence

• Matting marks: Every block examined at heights above 50 metres shows textile impressions from casting moulds — the weave pattern of the mould lining

• Uniform block composition: Chemical analysis shows blocks contain materials not found in nearby quarries, but consistent with a manufactured mixture

• Mould seams: The unfinished quartzite head of Nefertiti preserves a casting seam running down the facial centreline — forehead, nose, chin — visible proof it was cast in a two-part mould (chronologia.org Ch. 19 §7)

• Diorite vessels: Narrow-necked vessels of rock harder than iron, with uniform wall thickness and no tool marks — impossible to carve, trivial to cast on a potter's wheel

• No chisel jitter: Deep hieroglyphic inscriptions in hard granite show no tool deviation, even when crossing hard mineral inclusions — consistent with inscription in soft concrete before hardening

A Lost Technology

Geopolymer concrete was one of many technologies lost when the Horde Empire fragmented in the 17th century. Like Damascus steel, Rostov enamel, and tula granulation, the formula was a state secret known to a handful of craftsmen. When the Empire fell, the knowledge holders were killed or refused to share with conquerors. Alchemical texts encoding the recipe survived, but later generations — having lost the context — read them as mysticism. Davidovich's work rediscovered a technology that was never magic, only forgotten.

This Timeline's Position

Fomenko dates pyramid construction to the XIV–XVI centuries CE as part of the Horde's monumental building programme. This timeline accepts Davidovich's geopolymer evidence but disagrees with Fomenko's dating. The knowledge of geopolymer concrete was not a medieval alchemical invention — it was inherited from the Golden Age, carried into the Dark Ages by survivors of the cataclysm. Those who built the pyramids did so as early as necessary, applying techniques already perfected in an earlier epoch that Fomenko's framework does not account for. See Pyramids: Myths vs Reality for the full argument.

Deep State Redacts Christianity into Jesuit Catholicism

To control conquered territories, the Deep State needed a "slave" version of monotheism. Converting the Horde and its vassals away from Gnostic Christianity required supplanting it with Catholicism. Thus the Secret Society of Jesus was created.

The ancient world encoded theology in celestial movements. Christ's story is at core an astrological allegory for the Sun's annual passage through the twelve Zodiac houses. Key works: Dupuis, Origin of All Religious Worship (1798); Volney, The Ruins (1791); Taylor, The Diegesis (1829); Stowe, Bible Astrology (1907); Acharya S, The Christ Conspiracy (1999).

The Zodiac Ministry

Structural parallels: Sun reborn Dec. 25 (3 days after solstice "death") = Jesus born Dec. 25, resurrected after 3 days; Sun through 12 Zodiac houses = 12 disciples; Sun in Aquarius = baptism; Sun in Pisces = fishermen disciples; Sun in Aries = "Lamb of God"; Crucifixion at Vernal Equinox = Sun cross-ing the celestial equator; Resurrection = Sun ascending after spring equinox.

Scripture confirming Christ = Sun: "I am the light of the world" (John 8:12); "His face shone like the sun" (Matt 17:2); "Through him all things were made" (John 1:3).

Mo-Zeus and Je-Zeus: Jupiter Rebranded

Before redaction, the "Son of God" was visible every night as Jupiter. Greek Zeus and Roman Jupiter are the same figure; Mo-Zeus → Moses (radiant face, lawgiver = Jupiter's kingly function); Je-Zeus → Iesous → Jesus — the Greek form embeds Zeus directly.

What the "Slave Religion" Was

Gnostic Christians understood cosmic allegory: multiple divine principles, astronomical knowledge, Christ as principle (logos, light, reason) rather than literal person. The Jesuit-Catholic redaction inverted this: esoteric knowledge stripped from laity; solar cosmology reframed as "paganism"; observable sky worship replaced with obedience to invisible Rome-mediated authority; resistance = heresy = death.

As Galileo was told in 1632: "it would be necessary to forbid men to look at the heavens." That is not religion seeking truth — it is an information monopoly defending its franchise.

Canonical treatment: This article is the primary timeline treatment of Latin-as-control; other articles cross-reference here.

The Martian-origin model asks only that the civilization transmitting advanced knowledge also transmitted the indexing language. Latin is not dead. Revelation of the Coming Apocalypse

Revelation of the Method

"Realizing that our activities will sooner or later come to light, we structure our activities so that as conspiracy researchers unravel them, they will release information into the public consciousness in such a way that it mirrors our initiatory procedure... Success can then be measured precisely to the extent that our work is exposed."

Hoffman also writes:

"The purpose behind the 'Revelation of the Method' is the implied consent of the populace. If no resistance is raised when they reveal the truth, the cryptocracy is free to continue."

The Revelation Letters as RoM

• Identify friends and foes. Those who submit are allies; those who resist reveal themselves as targets.

• Demoralize opposition. When the enemy announces their plan and you cannot stop it, paralysis results. Many fled rather than fought.

• Establish perceived inevitability. Framing the plan as divine prophecy makes resistance blasphemous.

• Create plausible deniability. Once complete, the communiqués are reframed as "mystical prophecy" — which is exactly what happened when canonized into the Book of Revelation in the 18th century.

The strategy was highly effective. Those wishing to remain in Europe and avoid slaughter had to abandon ancient traditions and submit to the Greek Orthodox Church of the Rus-Horde Empire.

As documented in Deep State erases Russian Empire, Scaliger's chronology was political construction — manufacturing phantom centuries, inventing ancient civilizations, relocating events to suit a predetermined narrative.

Cathar Suppression

The Cathars practiced Gnostic Christianity based on Christ's mother Katya (Katherine). They spread West with the Rus-Horde Empire; after the religious schism, they were charged with heresy for denying the Divinity of Christ and Authority of the Pope and burned at the stake.

The full annihilation by the Reformation-era Deep State is covered in Cathars annihilated. This article covers Cathar identity and the XVI century Inquisition machinery. The eastern parallel (Kazan / Khazar) and the long arc to Reverse Crusades against Russia are in The Cathar–Khazar–Katya Connection.

The Name "Cathar" and Katherine Connection

Cathar derives from Greek katharoi — "the pure ones" — same root as catharsis, cauterize, and Catholic (katholikos = "Holy Katherine"). The institution that burned Cathars bore a name derived from the root it was destroying. Cathars claimed descent from Katya — Christ's mother in the NC framework — stripped from post-schism doctrine. To catharize meant to burn someone for Cathar faith.

Cathar Doctrine as Old-Empire Theology

Cathar doctrine preserved pre-schism unified Christianity in forms unacceptable to the post-Reformation Church: Dualism (light/matter); rejection of material sacraments (no transubstantiation — perfecti received direct spiritual transmission); celibate wandering preachers parallel to Orthodox missionaries; denial of Christ's physical suffering (original Docetist position: divine cannot be trapped in matter).

In NC, Cathars were not innovators — they were the most conservative remnant of the old faith. They were not heretics; they were the original Christians.

The Inquisition as Reversed Horde Cleansing

The original Inquisition was an imperial sanitation mechanism (see The Holy Inquisition). The "pure" (katharoi) were those who had not participated in the orgiastic cult — ascetics, celibates, wandering preachers. The Cathars, with perfecti structure and strict sexual renunciation, were exactly this population.

The Reversed Inquisition turned this machinery against the very people it originally protected — using it for ideological suppression during the Reformation.

Book of Judges, Joan of Arc, and Gilles de Rais

Fomenko & Nosovskiy identify the OT Book of Judges as the XVI century Inquisition's encoded record: Deborah ↔ Joan of Arc, Abimelech ↔ Simon de Montfort (Cathar crusade), Samson ↔ Gilles de Rais — all Inquisition-era cases folded into one biblical book. The full mapping, Joan’s trial parallels to Cathar charges, and the empire-wide compression argument are developed in Jeanne d'Arc is executed in Rouen, France.

What Was Lost

Systematic burning eliminated: the feminine principle (Katherine theology); direct revelation tradition; ascetic dualist framework; Gnostic Gospel tradition. What survived went underground — devotion to Mary Magdalene, troubadour tradition, chivalric secret societies.

See Deep State redacts Christianity for how solar religion was abstracted into invisible Rome-mediated deity.

10.07.00-gregorian-calendar-makes-slight.md

Gregorian Calendar Correction

In 1582, Pope Gregory XIII introduced the Gregorian calendar, ostensibly to correct a 10-day drift in the Julian calendar since the Council of Nicaea (325 CE). The reform deleted 10 days from October 1582 and adjusted the leap year rule — changing the average year from 365.25 to 365.2425 days. This created the Old Style/New Style split persisting today — Russia did not adopt the Gregorian calendar until 1918.

The Stated Reason vs. the True Reason

The official justification: the vernal equinox had drifted from March 21 to March 11, threatening the Paschal calendar's accuracy for computing Easter.

Fomenko and Nosovskiy demonstrate in Ch.7 §34 that the Paschal calendar (Computus) could not have been established at Nicaea in 325 CE:

The Council which established Paschal could not have taken place earlier than 784, as only beginning with this year, due to the slow astronomical shift of the moon phases, the concurrencies of the calendar Christian Easter and the Jewish Passover had ceased.

The fundamental rule — Easter must not coincide with Jewish Passover — would have been violated repeatedly in the IV century. The Computus was actually compiled no earlier than the VIII–XI centuries. The Gregorian reform corrected a much shorter drift while creating the false impression of continuous Church calendar use since the IV century.

The Scaligerian Year Zero

The reform was connected to Scaligerian chronology construction. The Anno Domini system was supposedly calculated by Dionysius Exiguus in the VI century. Fomenko notes:

"Since the beginning of A.D. there was no continuous yearly calculation until the current year. The first year 'A.D.' was calculated much later as the year of the Nativity of Christ."

Systematic Anno Domini use began only from 1431 — over 900 years after supposedly devised. Fomenko proposes "Dionysius the Small" (Exiguus) is a phantom of XVII century chronologist Dionysius Petavius (French petit = "small" → Petavius).

The Gregorian reform (1582), the Anno Domini era, and Scaliger's De emendatione temporum (1583) were simultaneous, coordinated operations replacing the Empire's existing calendar systems — including the Byzantine era (creation at 5508 BC) — with a framework pushing Christ ~1,053 years into the past and creating the illusion of unbroken Church history since antiquity.

What Was Replaced

The lunisolar calendar of the Rus-Horde Empire operated differently:

• 13 months of 28 days (with leap months) versus 12 months of uneven lengths

• Month names from natural phenomena — "Beginning of Spring," "Rain Water," "Pure Brightness" — versus months named after figures from fabricated Roman history: January after Janus, March after Mars, July after Julius Caesar (a redaction of Christ), August after Augustus Caesar

The Gregorian reform was not a technical adjustment but part of the broader project erecting the Scaligerian timeline, backdating the Church to the IV century, and erasing the Empire's calendrical systems.

This started real-world poverty never before seen on Earth.

To control the fractured Empire's shards, the Pyramidal Empire removed Rus-Horde from all history. The chronology compiled by Joseph Scaliger was heavily redacted and mass-redistributed by Jesuit Dionysius Petavius in Opus De Doctrina Temporum (1627). Fomenko writes:

Physical destruction extended to the most sacred sites. In Moscow's Cathedral of the Archangel — the Russian dynasty's burial site — the Romanovs carried out systematic erasure. Old tomb slabs were bricked over and re-plastered, new frescoes painted over originals. They created a fake Royal necropolis by relabeling existing graves, including converting nun tombstones into "Czar burials." The most telling evidence: burial type itself changed between 1632 and 1636. Prior Russian-Horde czars were interred using rites consistent with those in the Egyptian cemetery Western archaeologists would later call "Pharaonic." (Chronologia, Ch.7 §14)

Arabic on Russian Weapons

Among the most striking physical evidence: the Kremlin Armoury Chamber. As late as mid-17th century, Russian weapons bore Arabic inscriptions — including the "Jericho Cap" helmet of Czar Mikhail, engraved with Quranic verses. Paul of Aleppo wrote Russian in Arabic letters as late as 1656. Russian Imperial coins were bilingual — Khan's name on one side, Grand Prince's on the other, because they were the same person in two languages.

All of this dissolved after the Romanovs. Arabic was stripped, coins redesigned, bilingual records reclassified as "Tatar occupation" artifacts. (Chronologia, Ch.7 §8–10)

Foreign Occupation of Russian History

When Peter I founded the Russian Academy of Sciences in 1724, all historians were foreign for eighteen years — Germans, Dutch, Scandinavians constructing the "correct" past. Of thirty-four academics-historians between 1724 and 1841, only three were Russian. Foreign scholars controlled which documents were destroyed, rewritten, or preserved. Mikhail Lomonosov, the first Russian-born historian, recognized the deception and fought the foreign historians — a conflict he largely lost. (Chronologia, Ch.9 §2)

Great fight in the heart of Western Europe

The Thirty Years' War (1618–1648) was the final military campaign to permanently eliminate any possibility of Hordian Empire restoration in Central Europe. In the NC framework, this is cover: Westphalia was not a peace treaty but a carve-up agreement, dividing former Hordian provinces into permanently separate jurisdictions with incompatible legal systems, languages, and religions — the same cultural barrier construction being implemented simultaneously in chronology and coinage.

The Destruction of Cathar Castles and Hordian Fortifications

Per Fomenko (Ch.7 §11), "Cathar castles" of Southern France and Central European fortress complexes were not medieval relics of a 12th–13th century heresy — they were actual Hordian military fortifications. Gunpowder against stone castles is the critical tell: fortifications of the allegedly 13th century "Cathar Crusade" were destroyed with explosive charges unavailable in the 13th century. The English Revolution and Civil Wars

Protestants of Europe watched in dismay as the old Hordian Empire they despised was slowly Reformed into a dreadful 'Holy' Catholic Empire. Worse, English monarchy — whose vassal ties were long cut from Russian Empire — still behaved as if in charge, claiming divine right to rule. Many rejected this, debating power transfer from monarchy to Parliament.

On Jan 4th 1642, Charles I entered House of Commons with 400 soldiers attempting to arrest 5 members for treason — they escaped. Widespread conflict broke out between Parliamentarians and Royalists, continuing 10 years. In 1649, Charles was found guilty of high treason as "tyrant, traitor, murderer and public enemy" and beheaded at Whitehall. The regicide caused England to become a Republic, or Commonwealth, lasting until 1660 Stuart Restoration. Of 59 Commissioners who signed death warrant, 9 were executed, many died in prison.

The Batih Massacre: mass ethnic cleansing of Russian captives after Battle of Batih, 3–4 June 1652 near Ladyzhyn (now Ukraine), carried out by Ukrainian Cossacks under Bohdan Khmelnytsky.

NC View of English History

In NC framework, English Civil War was not merely constitutional dispute but part of global Reformation campaign to transfer Hordian Empire assets from old aristocracy to new financial class. Fomenko's analysis (Ch.7 §18): written English history concerning events specifically on British Isles only becomes reliable in 16th–17th century. Everything earlier is mixture of genuine insular records with Romaic (Byzantine) and Hordian chronicles transferred to England during Horde conquest, then re-edited during Reformation to appear as native English history.

The Reformers had strategic reason for choosing England:

"It is possible that the Reformers moved the stolen treasury of the Empire to England. They didn't want to take a chance of keeping it in Europe, weary of the restoration of Russia-Horde. At first they tried to create a new metropoly in Vienna, Austria by installing their string puppets there under the pompous name of the 'Habsburgs'. Nothing came of it. That is why the Hordian money was taken as far away as possible to the distant English isles."
— Fomenko, Ch.7 §18

This reframes the English Civil War entirely. Charles I was not abstract tyrant resisting parliamentary democracy but representative of older aristocratic order — remnant of Hordian governance — confronting financial revolution that had already toppled dynasties across Europe.

Societized Consolidation: The Template for Western Governance

The Civil War was more than a constitutional dispute. It was the first Western European experiment in societized consolidation — transferring resources and legitimacy from Crown and Church to institutions claiming to represent "the people." Crown lands were sequestered and sold. Bishops were abolished (1646); Church lands liquidated. Royal prerogative over taxation, army, and succession was permanently broken.

This produced the political template replicated in the French Revolution (1789) and every Western state since:

• Meritocracy: The Self-denying Ordinance (1645) removed aristocratic commanders; Cromwell rose on competence, not birth

• Plutocracy: Property qualifications for political participation (Instrument of Government, 1653)

• Particracy: Governance by faction — Parliamentarians vs. Royalists; later Whigs vs. Tories

• Electoral autocracy: Cromwell as Lord Protector — strong executive with popular mandate, overriding monarchical legitimacy

The Commonwealth was the first openly money-governed state in the Western world. Bank of England, founded forty-five years after Charles's execution, completed the project. The Civil War reorganized the English state before imperial expansion — Parliament's control over taxation, the navy, and foreign policy created the institutional prerequisites for the British Empire.

The Scots as Scythians

Per Fomenko (Ch.7 §3), Scots — Scythians in origin — occupied uniquely privileged position in Hordian nobility of England. In Reformation-era Continental Europe, the same ethnic group England called "noble Scots" was labeled "filthy Scythian beasts" in Romanov-occupied Russia. The same word — Scythian — carried opposite social valences depending on which side of Reformation divide. This dual treatment of Scythian/Cossack class is marker of how comprehensively 17th century rewrote Empire's social history.

See: British Civil War Investigation for full evidence on societized consolidation, the 1666 apocalyptic sequence, and the connection to the French Revolution.

Marfull Framework

The Marfull chronological framework — developed by researchers building on Fomenko's New Chronology — provides a parallel reconstruction of 17th century events focused on the Mediterranean theatre: the Catalan-Aragonese military campaigns, the inter-religious disputes between Judaism and Christianity, the dismantling of the Templar military-financial order, and the consolidation of Roman papal authority over the competing Avignon papacy.

The key insight of the Marfull framework is the chronological compression: events conventionally dated to the 13th–15th centuries (the Cathar Crusades, the Templar suppression, the Tortosa Dispute) are identified as 17th century events that were backdated by the Scaligerian chronological system. The equation 1307 = 1492 = 1677 — asserting that the Templar suppression, the Spanish expulsion of Jews, and a 17th century restructuring are the same event recorded at three different apparent dates — is one of the most striking claims in the New Chronology reconstruction.

These Mediterranean events were not isolated from the Northern European drama of the Romanov seizure and the English financial coup. They represent the southern front of the same global restructuring: the transfer of military, financial, and religious authority from the old Imperial order to the new system of competing nation-states governed by an invisible financial network.

Per the Marfull chronological framework, the inter-religious disputes between Judaism and Christianity spanning 1633–1725 CE were the theological dimension of a political transformation — the creation of separate, controllable religious denominations out of what had been a unified Imperial religious tradition.

1673 – Disputes end (46 years): The Tortosa Dispute (1413–14 in Scaligerian dating) — 70+ rabbis vs. Geronimo de Santa Fe before Pope Benedict XIII. Mass conversions; Pope Luna's resignation transferred all religious authority to Rome.

1677 – Templar dismantling: The equation 1307 = 1492 = 1677 — Templar suppression, Spanish expulsion of Jews, and 17th c. restructuring as the same event. Creation of the Order of Saint John (1312 = 1497 = 1682) replaced Templars under Roman papacy; Prester John camouflages the Hordian Czar/Khan.

Disputes against Jews reemerge

The breaking up of the Russian Empire created the Jewish and Gypsy / Romani diasporas. Both groups had traveled freely between vassal states and now found themselves cut off from old imperial ties. Jews found themselves at the highest level of authority in each region — controlling money, trade, and monarchies — while Gypsies, named after an Empire that no longer existed, became powerless. Disputes between common folk (gentiles) and Jews emerged over every facet of life, from eating rituals to monetary policy.

The Imperial Treasury Caste

The New Chronology offers a structural explanation for Jewish survival after the Empire's collapse. In the Hordian Empire, social organization followed a hereditary caste model. The financial officers — managing accounts across Europe, Africa, and America, collecting taxes, controlling precious metal flows, financing the army — formed a distinct professional caste. Their work demanded precision and a strict regulatory mindset (hence the Talmudic legal system's emphasis on financial rules).

This caste operated globally by necessity: an Imperial banker in Egypt and one in England had stronger professional ties than two soldiers in distant provinces. Over generations, this tight-knit guild developed its own religious community, becoming a recognizable strand of what we now call Judaism. Crucially, this guild needed no territorial state — their "country" was the Empire itself. When the Empire collapsed, they retained cosmopolitan mobility and professional networks, while warrior castes and administrative nobles were territorialized and eventually replaced. (Chronologia, Ch.9 §3)

Power of Money Erupts

Fomenko identifies the critical transition: in the 16th–17th centuries, as the Empire fractured, the previously subordinate financial caste found itself in unprecedented power. Military governors of new independent states believed they were the victors. They were not. The driving force behind the revolutionary wave sweeping Europe (English Revolution, French Revolution, Dutch Republic) was capital overriding nobility — the Miser Knight pattern: the Imperial knight demanding salary from the treasury official who now controlled the cash.

"The driving force behind all such revolutions was money. As a result they openly declared the domination of money over noble rank, over ancestry. This was the motto of the French revolution and of the English revolution. In the Horde Empire there ruled a principle of ancestry and gentility. Nobility was respected and entitled to power. After the revolt of the Reformation, wealth acquired precedence." — Fomenko, Ch.9 §3

The ghettos appearing across Europe after 1615 were not continuation of ancient prejudice but a new phenomenon — response to the Empire's collapse whose treasury system the Jewish community had administered. Once Imperial authority was gone, local kingdoms had no framework for integrating former Imperial officials. A 1615 Lutheran Chronograph records the expulsion of Jews from Worms directly after the Time of Troubles. (Chronologia, Ch.9 §3)
