Central Intelligence Agency: OSS Rebrand and Cold War Deep State

The Central Intelligence Agency (CIA) was created by the National Security Act of 1947, formally replacing and expanding the wartime Office of Strategic Services (OSS). Conventional history treats it as the civilian foreign-intelligence arm of the United States. This timeline treats its origins, funding, and long-term role as an open investigation: OSS rebrand, alleged appropriation of Nazi gold and assets, creation largely outside American constitutional and republican oversight, and operation as a self-funding structure—drug trade, human trafficking, and covert commerce—that grew into what critics describe as a massive parasite on the state.

OSS to CIA: Continuity, Not Clean Break

The OSS (1942–1945) ran espionage, sabotage, and resistance coordination; many of its personnel and networks flowed directly into the CIA. The “rebrand” was legal and bureaucratic, not operational. Pre-1947 activity—Nazi tracking, asset recovery, and British (MI6) cooperation—shaped the Agency’s culture and methods. Allegations that OSS/CIA elements captured or redirected Nazi gold and other assets during and after the war are documented in the CIA Investigation.

Outside the Constitution

The CIA was given broad authorities and minimal public accountability: no declaration of war for its operations, no transparent line-item for much of its budget, and no electorate to answer to. The investigation examines the thesis that it was designed to operate outside the normal constraints of a republic—funding and enriching itself off the books while being protected by classification, disinformation, and institutional loyalty.

Iran: Shah, Nukes, Ayatollah

The Agency’s hand in Iran is well documented for the 1950s: Operation Ajax (1953), restoration of the Shah, and support for nuclear-related infrastructure. The investigation traces the thesis that the same Anglo-American intelligence cooperation that built up the Shah and nuclear stations later helped engineer conditions for the Ayatollah to take over—so that nuclear assets would fall into the hands of radical extremists, creating an existential narrative and a possible “revelation of the method” (CIA-declassified intelligence suggesting handover of nuclear weapons to terrorist proxies) to justify a global end-game. The investigation also records the author's opinion: that the world has been blind to the Iranian nuclear threat above all because of the fatwa (nuclear bombs declared haram)—everyone assumes Iranians would never break it—while the real risk is depleted uranium (stored as "peaceful" byproduct), widely misunderstood and weaponisable with minimal technology, and that the CIA may have set up an underground facility to convert it. See CIA Investigation.

Latin America: Gangs, Noriega, Maduro

Over decades, the CIA is alleged to have built or empowered criminal and paramilitary networks across Latin America—removing “good” actors and leaving “bad” ones in place—so that figures like Noriega (Bush Sr.) and Maduro (Trump-era) became targets of direct US action. The pattern is read as a long-term deep-state project with the Agency at the centre; the struggle to reverse it is read as within government, between old bureaucracy and “antibodies.”

Antibodies: Defeat from Within

The only effective resistance, in this reading, came from antibodies—insiders who turned on the plan. Daniel Ellsberg (Pentagon Papers) is the exemplar: after participating in the system, he sought political cover and disclosure, helping to create space for scrutiny and new agencies. The investigation documents the thesis that the CIA was ultimately defeated by people inside the CIA who no longer wanted to be part of it and who used documents and political cover to create or empower new structures.

9/11: Disconnecting the CIA from Its Plans

This timeline sponsors a good-actor thesis for 9/11: that the attack was used—or executed—against the Deep State. False Flags: Architecture: good actors pre-empted a slow-burn Holy War; WTC 7 (which housed a CIA secret station) was destroyed; the War on Terror was made public, not covert. Claims that the NSA or allied actors “took out” the CIA by bringing down Building 7, and post-9/11 statements by CIA representatives that the Agency was diminished, are collected in the CIA Investigation. The thesis: 9/11 was triggered early on purpose to disconnect the CIA from its long-term plans; new agencies under the War on Terror would “stomp out” those plans over time, in the US and abroad.

Post-9/11 Crises: Tracing to the CIA

Almost every major American crisis since 9/11—from near–civil war and near-insurrection to government capture by media and institutional narratives (e.g. under the guise of science and vaccines)—is under investigation for traceability to CIA-linked elements. The investigation will map personnel, programmes, and narrative production to the Agency where evidence allows.

The Soviet Collapse

The collapse of the Soviet Union is conventionally attributed to internal failure — economic stagnation, nationalist movements, Gorbachev's miscalculations. The timeline framework identifies it as largely orchestrated by the West through four decades of deliberate isolation, media warfare, espionage, and celebrity soft-power penetration.

The Pretended Alliance

The Western alliance with Russia in WWII was tactical, not genuine. The West needed the Red Army to defeat the Wehrmacht; once Germany fell, the USSR was immediately reclassified as the next target:

• 1946: Churchill's "Iron Curtain" speech

• 1947: Truman Doctrine; Marshall Plan excludes USSR

• 1949: NATO founded; COCOM export controls begin

Decades of isolation denied the Soviet bloc access to Western capital, technology, and markets — forcing autarky and comparative disadvantage. This was not passive containment; it was engineered decline.

Media Warfare and Celebrity Penetration

Western media shaped global perception of Soviet life as drab, oppressive, and backward:

• VOA, Radio Free Europe, BBC World Service: Decades of broadcasts into the Soviet bloc providing alternative narratives and amplifying dissent

• Glasnost-era access: Gorbachev's reforms allowed Western media in; the regime lost control of the narrative

A striking case: Michael Palin (BBC's Pole to Pole) was filming in the USSR during the August 1991 coup — the exact moment hardliners attempted to seize power from Gorbachev. A beloved British comedian with a BBC crew, operating inside Soviet territory during its most volatile three days. Palin himself noted that as he left the Soviet Union to cross into Turkey, the countries he had just passed through were declaring independence behind him — creating, in his words, a sense that he was "riding the wave of revolution." Whether or not the production had direct intelligence function, the narrative effect was significant: Western audiences saw the USSR disintegrate through a Western lens, narrated by a familiar, trusted face.

Espionage and "Democracy Promotion"

• CIA and MI6: Long-standing operations supporting anti-Soviet dissidents, penetrating institutions, funding opposition

• NED (National Endowment for Democracy): Founded 1983 — semi-public regime-change arm funding "democracy promotion" across Eastern Europe

• Solidarity template (Poland): Western support for the Polish trade union that challenged Soviet hegemony — rehearsal for later interventions

These operations amplified internal weaknesses and ensured that when the regime cracked, the West had assets and narratives in place to shape the outcome.

Connection to the Reverse Crusades

The Soviet collapse is the delayed victory of the 1941 Reverse Crusade. When Operation Barbarossa failed to destroy Russia militarily, the strategy shifted to economic isolation, cultural penetration, and patient destabilization. It took fifty years, but the result was the same: Russia's state structures dismantled, its archives partially opened (enabling Fomenko's New Chronology to reach a wider audience), and its resources targeted for Western extraction under Yeltsin.

See: Soviet Collapse Investigation for full evidence on Western orchestration, media operations, and the Michael Palin case.

The entire architecture depended on one precondition: a unified Western bloc willing to escalate in lockstep. Every NATO member had to maintain sanctions. Every Western media outlet had to maintain the narrative. Every Western government had to funnel weapons without public dissent. The alliance cascade — the lesson of WW1 — only works if every node in the network fires when triggered.

Europe's War / America's Escalation

By 2025, Europe's escalation rested on six individuals (Macron, Merz, Starmer, von der Leyen, Kallas, Rutte) — none holding positions through broad popular mandate for war. The U.S. side — Biden, Harris, Blinken, Austin, Nuland, Sullivan, Graham, McConnell — was equally concentrated and bipartisan. In November 2024, Biden authorised ATACMS strikes into Russian territory after losing the election; the margin between proxy war and nuclear exchange was one election. For full operator profiles and escalation timeline: 21st Century Geopolitics: Extended in the Appendix.

Why Trump Broke the Plan

This is why Trump broke the plan.

Trump's return to power shattered the unified Western front that the WW3 blueprint required. A U.S. president who questioned NATO's purpose, demanded European defence spending, pursued direct communication with Russia, and refused to follow the escalation script made the cascade impossible. The architecture was built for a Clinton, a Biden, a Macron — leaders who would follow the playbook without deviation. It was not built for an antibody who understood the script and refused to play his part.

Without a unified West, the Sarajevo-style trigger cannot activate. Without unified media, the Zelensky brand cannot be maintained. Without unified escalation, the proxy war becomes a frozen conflict rather than a continental conflagration. The WW3 blueprint — the most sophisticated version of the Reverse Crusade, combining the best innovations of 1914 and 1941 — was structurally defeated by the one variable it could not control: an uncompromised leader inside the alliance who refused to fire when triggered.

For the full structural comparison of all four Reverse Crusades, see Reverse Crusades Investigation.

The Disclosure Pattern

The Pyramidal Empire operates by "Revelation of the Method" — announcing plans publicly before executing them, obtaining implied consent from a population that fails to resist. In the 21st century this has accelerated to absurdity: pandemic simulations months before pandemics, financial collapse simulations before collapses, fictional portrayals of technologies before deployment.

But the tool now works against them. The population recognizes "predictive programming" so readily that disclosure no longer functions as consent — it functions as warning. This may be the Pyramidal Empire's single greatest tactical loss.

The Timeline Converges

Every chapter traces a narrowing path toward stark possibilities:

• Total control: global digital governance and permanent surveillance before any cosmic event disrupts it

• Collapse: Pyramidal Empire fragments, taking global infrastructure with it, leaving humanity unprepared

• Controlled transition: enough antibodies survive to guide humanity through catastrophe while preserving sovereignty

• Awakening: mass recognition of the false timeline renders control structures obsolete

15.02.00-20th-c-predictive-programming.md
========================================================================

20th C. Predictive Programming

The decades following Operation Mockingbird saw the industrialisation of a technique far older than the CIA: reframing real events as fiction. From the 1890s onward, authors—some conscious operatives, some cultural carriers—systematically converted suppressed history into “fantasy” and “science fiction.” The effect was erasure. Once a real event becomes a novel, anyone who later discovers the truth sounds as if they are describing a plot summary.

This is the backward direction of predictive programming: not announcing future operations, but burying past ones under copyright-protected fiction.

Comparisons between encoded fiction and “real history” usually assume the conventional chronology. If that timeline is wrong, shifted, or partly synthetic, control events will not line up with narrative echoes: mismatches then look like coincidence or overreading rather than failed proof. Mass redaction and chronology fights mean the public record is a thin witness; fiction may still carry displaced residue of what was erased, while rigorous demonstration stays blocked until the comparator calendar is questioned or rebuilt. On this line of work, a working prediction holds that scattered investigations—each fragile under official dates—could converge toward harder-to-dismiss patterns once a reconstructed or alternate timeline is accepted as the baseline for comparison, not as automatic fact but as the yardstick. See history / chronology, the predictive programming hub (approach item on chronology as comparator), and the George R. R. Martin / World of Ice & Fire investigation (section Chronology as the missing comparator).

H. G. Wells set an early template with The Crystal Egg (scrying), The War of the Worlds (heat ray, red weed), and the Plattner and Davidson stories (dimensional and remote-viewing motifs). He was likely compromised: reputation sabotage and unprofitable fiction for Macmillan point toward serving powerful interests; see compromised court cases. Arthur Machen’s The Great God Pan tied brain surgery to dimensional perception; The Angels of Mons showed fiction passing as news. Algernon Blackwood’s The Willows and John Silence treated thought as a surveillance surface. H. P. Lovecraft wrote roughly 104 stories that encode scrying, remote viewing, and telepathic broadcast. Edgar Rice Burroughs’s Barsoom offered polar conspiracies and telepathic technology. J. R. R. Tolkien used the Red Book as pseudotranslation, assembled The Silmarillion as systematic reworking of older matter, and left copyright that runs until 2043 and beyond.

William Goldman’s The Princess Bride (1973) belongs to a later phase: the Morgenstern frame, the father’s oral cuts and “good parts” abridgement (redaction narrated as fiction), and the mask dialogue about everyone wearing them “in the near future.” By then the Second World War was long past, and public memory of the sharper fights over myth-making and documentary-framed fiction associated with Wells and Tolkien had thinned. A working conclusion ties Goldman-style success to institutional favors and mass-publishing gatekeeping. A further working conclusion (very likely on strategic logic, unproven bibliographically) treats S. Morgenstern as probably a real or once-real authorial line whose complete edition could no longer surface, which would have made the abridgement gambit safe and opened space for historic fantasy as repackaged copyright fiction.

That strategic picture raises a mechanism question: ordinary obscurity rarely justifies confidence that every edition of a book has vanished. Earlier, in May 1933, Nazi Germany staged massive public book burnings—iconic events across German cities, including Berlin’s Bebelplatz—that destroyed large quantities of targeted works in the open; a hypothetical Morgenstern line in German libraries or trade could have disappeared there too (Nazi book burnings). One historically documented class of mass removals after the war is the purging of defeated-regime literature—German denazification is the familiar label. In Italy, Allied Order No. 4 (signed 13 May 1946) ordered removal and destruction of fascist, militarist, and Nazi literature from libraries, schools, and bookstores—administrative book destruction, not slow market fade. Other former Axis zones underwent parallel Allied and successor controls that could strip whole categories of print from legal trade and public shelves. A working hypothesis (no list naming Morgenstern has been located in the linked investigation) holds that a real author line later tagged as “S. Morgenstern” might have been caught in such a sweep, so that by the late 1960s or early 1970s whoever cleared the frame could gamble that no checkable original would reappear—making a fictional Morgenstern abridgement effectively non-falsifiable in public catalogues (“perfect crime” shape in publishing terms). See Princess Bride investigation Key findings §5 (coda) and section Book destruction (1933, 1946) and the “perfect crime” window.

The same frame assumes the audience shares a child’s impatience: kingdoms, customs, reasons, and Morgenstern’s satiric history are boring unless cut to action. The reader is aligned with the boy who wants entertainment, not inquiry; torture, court politics, and war are delivered as thrills rather than as material that belongs in open historical judgment. A working reading holds that this posture models mass audience as anti-curious—amusement over substance—while the “dull” layers are exactly where redaction can hide. See the Goldman subsection in Predictive Programming: Literature — Extended, the full Princess Bride investigation (Key findings §6; section Anti-intellectual frame: reader as the child who skips the real book). Orson Scott Card’s Ender’s Game (1985) put planetary destruction inside “simulations” that were real battles and landed on the U.S. Marine Corps Reading List.

1942–1992 CE — Asimov: Marx in Space!

Isaac Asimov’s Three Laws of Robotics read as slave codes, and the Foundation cycle retells Marx in space. For the full analysis, see Asimov: Three Laws and Marx Encoding — Extended.

Each author in this cluster converts suppressed knowledge into protected fiction. Wells in the 1890s exemplifies Hordian erasure; Lovecraft in the interwar years fits Mockingbird consolidation; Tolkien marks a phase of formalised literary rights; Card sits near the Cold War peak. For extended author-by-author evidence, see Predictive Programming: Literature — Extended.

Literature supplied the templates; film and television and games scaled them. For that branch of the pattern, see Predictive Programming in Film, Television, and Games.

15.05.00-population-control-from-1492-to.md

Population Control: From 1492 to the Present

The Georgia Guidestones, erected in 1980 in rural Georgia, inscribed in eight languages a directive to "maintain humanity under 500,000,000 in perpetual balance with nature." The stones were destroyed in 2022 — but the programme they announced has operated for centuries. Population control is the recurring Deep State mechanism to periodically reduce human numbers, restore order, or engineer cultural revolution when populations exceed manageability.

The pattern predates the Guidestones by half a millennium. For the full chronological dossier from 1492 through the 21st century, see Population Control: A Timeline (15th–21st Centuries) in the Appendix.

Modern Forms: Suppression and Poisoning

Research into Tennant and the Flexner Report demonstrates that Rockefeller and allied foundations systematically suppressed health, medicine, and science that could extend life and cure chronic disease. The mechanism: defund non-compliant schools, criminalize electrotherapy, enforce drug-and-surgery-only paradigms. The result — generations denied bioelectric healing, nutrition-based recovery, and energy medicine — constitutes suppressed science as population control.

Direct poisoning operates in parallel: adulterated food, environmental pollution, questionable vaccine mandates, and harmful radiation (from medical over-imaging to EMF saturation) produce attrition without declared war. Food shortage and engineered starvation, collapse of bee populations and ecosystem degradation, solar-dimming proposals to block crops, and herbicide spraying that kills or contaminates harvests — the Gaza conflict exemplifies attrition through denial of food, water, and medicine. All produce population reduction under plausible deniability.

Body and Family: Making Resistance Impossible

Modern population control extends beyond death into dependency and surrender of reproduction. People are encouraged to forgo families, voluntarily sterilize, and treat children as burdens. Body modification — piercings, tattoos, surgical augmentation, and emerging chip/hardware implants — produces a more controllable society: populations bound to master technology, like the lower classes in H.G. Wells's War of the Worlds, reduced to servile dependency on those who control the machines.

The end goal: a point where resistance is not only impossible but unthinkable. A population that has given up sovereignty, reproduction, and bodily integrity cannot mount effective opposition.

For Children of Men (2006) as predictive programming of population collapse, see Predictive Programming in Film, Television, and Games.

15.06.00-2009-ce-cern-s-lhc-portal.md
========================================================================

CERN's LHC & Portal Technology

In November 2009, Sergio Bertolucci — CERN's Director for Research and Scientific Computing — briefed reporters at CERN HQ on the Large Hadron Collider. He stated that the LHC might create or discover "previously unimagined scientific phenomena," including "an extra dimension," and said:

"Out of this door might come something, or we might send something through it."

The words are on the record. No other explanation for the LHC's role fits the combination of that statement, the physics of the medium, and a century of suppressed aether research. This timeline treats CERN's LHC as a portal — or portal-capable device — in which the "fabric" that particle physics calls the quantum vacuum (and that pre-20th-century physics called the aether) is manipulated at scale. Evidence of this kind — a senior director using door/through language in an official briefing — is gold.

Why the Portal Reading Fits

•
The Suppression of Maxwell and the Aether: If the aether is real and manipulable, portal technology is an engineering problem. The LHC concentrates extreme energies and fields in a confined volume; in aether physics that is local distortion of the medium — i.e. portal-capable. Mainstream physics already describes the vacuum as a quantum field, not emptiness; the LHC is therefore manipulating a medium. The taboo is against the word "aether," not the concept.

•
19th Century Weapons and the Declassification Thesis: Knowledge that could not be openly weaponised or monetised was classified. Aether-based transport or gateways would fit that pattern. CERN's public narrative ("we only do particle physics") does not discharge the burden of proof; the Bertolucci quote stands as primary-source evidence that door/portal language was used at the highest level.

•
Predictive programming: Every major LHC milestone (first beams 2008, restart 2009, Higgs 2012, restart 2022) has been accompanied by a wave of claims that CERN "opened a portal" or "let something through." Fact-checkers cite CERN denials and close the question. Per our investigative strategy, we treat that pattern as data — and the primary source (Bertolucci) as decisive for the portal interpretation.

What the Investigation Contains

The full evidentiary record — Bertolucci in context, Reuters 2010 on extra dimensions, the Shiva/Nataraja statue at CERN (2004), official denials and fact-checks, and the aether–LHC link — is in the investigations folder. Timeline article is summary and stance; evidence and sources live there.

See: CERN and Portal Technology (investigation).

Cross-References

• Operation Mockingbird — narrative control around CERN and "portal" claims

15.08.00-suppression-of-wuxing-four-elements-replacement.md
========================================================================

Suppression of Wuxing — Four Elements, Air, and the Erasure of Planetary Science

Wuxing — the Five Phases rooted in the Dark Ages observation of five planets and their causal interactions — was suppressed in mainland China under CCP communism and replaced in the West by a different system: four elements, with air included as an element even though it is not one in the Wuxing framework. The replacement eliminated the nature of causality between elements, the science, the history, and the link between the five phases and the five bodies that had once been visible in the sky.

CCP Suppression and the Bruce Lee Legend

In China, traditions that preserved Wuxing as a living practice — including lineages of female martial artists who had operated and transmitted it for over 300 years — were targeted under communist cultural policy. The story of Bruce Lee is often tied to these legends: he is said to have taken or carried the legends of those female martial artists and the Wuxing-based arts into the West. Suddenly, in China, the living tradition was gone: suppressed, scattered, or driven underground. In the West, what arrived was fragmentary — and the dominant Western framework was already the Greek-derived four elements (earth, air, fire, water), not five. Western insistence on four elements and on air as an element overwrote the five-phase system. Causality between elements, the observational basis, and the planetary link were dropped; Wuxing was treated as philosophy or superstition, with no real science behind it.

Why Forgetting Was Easy — After the Dark Ages

Once the solar system had stabilized and the planets had departed to their current orbits, the conditions that had made Wuxing obvious disappeared. The sensitivities of the meteorologists — the ability to read sky and space weather — faded. There was no more space weather in the old sense, no more cataclysm, no more visible interplanetary causality. With nothing in the sky to anchor the five phases, it became easy for everyone to forget Wuxing as science and to treat it only as philosophy, hypothetical, something with no real basis. Suppression under the CCP and replacement by the four-element model in the West then completed the erasure.

Wuxing as Solar-System Science

This timeline argues that Wuxing is essentially the science of our solar system — not because five is a magic number, but because that is how things turned out here. We had five planets in the relevant configuration and five causalities. In a solar system with a different number of dominant bodies (a scenario that may not be all that possible elsewhere), things would be different: colors, the number of digits on a hand, and much else would align to whatever number that system settled on. As energy flows through the planets, the high scalarity of five planets sharing energy at the same time breaks down into micro-scalarity and affects every life form in the solar system. Wuxing, in that reading, is the codified record of that scalar structure — and its suppression is the suppression of that science.

The Real Nature of Wuxing — Combinations, Not Pure Elements

A crucial distinction: in the original Wuxing framework, the planets were regarded as the elements themselves — not as “pure” fire, pure water, or the like. Each planet was a combination of all elements, with one element as the head. Nothing else is asserted: Wuxing does not claim that “fire” and “water” as abstract absolutes do such-and-such. It claims that these planets did things, and that the interactions between planets gave clues about the lower-level elements — fire, water, earth, metal, wood. The real nature of Wuxing is therefore combination of elements with one element at the head — a planetary, observational fact — not a set of claims about pure substances. The four-element replacement, and the insertion of “air,” erased that structure and with it the causality and the history.

Author's thesis: The suppression of Wuxing under the CCP and its replacement in the West by the four elements (including air) removed the last widespread link between the five-phase system and its observational, planetary basis. Restoring Wuxing means restoring it as the science of a five-planet configuration — and as a system of combinations with one element at the head, not as a philosophy of pure elements.

See also: Wuxing and the Five Planets; Censorship and Book Burning.
